L "# CNN FALLBACK (ForgeNet)"
T GRU
L "# Trained online using rewards shaped by WorldModel priors."
T GAA
L "class ForgeNet(nn.Module):"
L "    def __init__(s, in_ch=26, g=64):"
L "        super().__init__()"
L "        s.c1 = nn.Conv2d(in_ch, 32, 3, padding=1)"
L "        s.c2 = nn.Conv2d(32, 64, 3, padding=1)"
L "        s.c3 = nn.Conv2d(64, 128, 3, padding=1)"
L "        s.ap = nn.MaxPool2d(4, 4)"
L "        s.af = nn.Linear(128 * 16 * 16, 256)"
L "        s.ah = nn.Linear(256, 5)"
L "        s.cc1 = nn.Conv2d(128, 64, 3, padding=1)"
L "        s.cc2 = nn.Conv2d(64, 1, 1)"
L ""
L "    def forward(s, x):"
T GNP
L "        af = s.ap(f).reshape(f.size(0), -1)"
L "        al = s.ah(F.relu(s.af(af)))"
T GOP
L "        return torch.cat([al, cl], 1)"
L ""

L "# MAIN AGENT"
T GAA

T GAA

L ""
L "def to_2d(obs_or_array):"
L "    if obs_or_array is None:"
L "        return np.zeros((64, 64), dtype=np.int32)"
L "    if hasattr(obs_or_array, 'frame'):"
L "        f = np.array(obs_or_array.frame, dtype=np.int32)"
L "    else:"
L "        f = np.asarray(obs_or_array, dtype=np.int32)"
L "    if f.ndim == 3:"
L "        f = f[-1]"
L "    if f.ndim != 2:"
L "        f = np.zeros((64, 64), dtype=np.int32)"
L "    return f"
L ""
L "def bg_color(frame):"
L "    return int(np.bincount(np.asarray(frame).flatten()).argmax())"
L ""
L "def bfs_path(grid, start, goal, walls):"
L "    if start == goal:"
L "        return []"
L "    h, w = grid.shape"
L "    q, vis = deque([(start, [])]), {start}"
L "    deltas = {1:(-1,0), 2:(1,0), 3:(0,-1), 4:(0,1)}"
L "    while q:"
L "        pos, path = q.popleft()"
L "        for act, (dr, dc) in deltas.items():"
L "            n = (pos[0] + dr, pos[1] + dc)"
L "            if 0 <= n[0] < h and 0 <= n[1] < w and n not in vis and not walls[n[0], n[1]]:"
L "                vis.add(n)"
L "                new_path = path + [act]"
L "                if n == goal:"
L "                    return new_path"
L "                q.append((n, new_path))"
L "    return []"
L ""
L "def lawnmower_scan(w=64, h=64, step=8):"
L "    pos = []"
L "    for i, y in enumerate(range(0, h, step)):"
L "        xs = range(0, w, step) if i % 2 == 0 else range(w - 1, -1, -step)"
L "        for x in xs:"
L "            pos.append((x, y))"
L "    return pos"
L ""
L "def gf2_solve(toggle_matrix, target_state):"
L "    n, m = len(target_state), len(toggle_matrix)"
L "    if m == 0 or n == 0:"
L "        return []"
L "    aug = np.zeros((n, m + 1), dtype=int)"
L "    for i in range(n):"
L "        for j in range(m):"
L "            aug[i, j] = int(toggle_matrix[j][i]) % 2"
L "        aug[i, m] = int(target_state[i]) % 2"
L "    pivot_cols, row = [], 0"
L "    for col in range(m):"
L "        found = next((r for r in range(row, n) if aug[r, col] == 1), -1)"
L "        if found == -1:"
L "            continue"
L "        aug[[row, found]] = aug[[found, row]]"
L "        for r in range(n):"
L "            if r != row and aug[r, col] == 1:"
L "                aug[r] = (aug[r] + aug[row]) % 2"
L "        pivot_cols.append(col)"
L "        row += 1"
L "    sol = np.zeros(m, dtype=int)"
L "    for i, col in enumerate(pivot_cols):"
L "        if i < n:"
L "            sol[col] = aug[i, m]"
L "    return [j for j in range(m) if sol[j] == 1]"
L ""
L "def detect_player_pos(prev_frame, cur_frame):"
L "    diff = (prev_frame != cur_frame)"
L "    if not diff.any():"
L "        return None"
L "    bg = bg_color(prev_frame)"
L "    rows, cols = np.where(diff)"
L "    for r, c in zip(rows, cols):"
L "        if prev_frame[r, c] == bg and cur_frame[r, c] != bg:"
L "            return (int(r), int(c))"
L "    return (int(rows.mean()), int(cols.mean()))"
L ""
L "class GameObserver:"
L "    def __init__(self):"
L "        self.jsonl = []"
L "        self.action_eff = defaultdict(list)"
L "        self.click_effs = []"
L "        self.toggle_map = {}"
L "        self.player_trail = []"
L "        self.player_color = None"
L "        self._bg = None"
L ""
L "    def start_phase(self, frame, avail):"
L "        self._bg = bg_color(frame)"
L ""
L "    def record(self, prev_frame, action, data, cur_frame, step, obs2=None):"
L "        changed = not np.array_equal(prev_frame, cur_frame)"
L "        diff_count = int(np.sum(prev_frame != cur_frame))"
L "        bg = self._bg if self._bg is not None else bg_color(prev_frame)"
L "        self.action_eff[action].append({'changed': changed, 'diff': diff_count})"
L ""
L "        if action in [1, 2, 3, 4] and changed:"
L "            diff_mask = (prev_frame != cur_frame)"
L "            appeared = {"
L "                int(cur_frame[r, c]) for r, c in zip(*np.where(diff_mask))"
L "                if prev_frame[r, c] == bg and cur_frame[r, c] != bg"
L "            }"
L "            disappeared = {"
L "                int(prev_frame[r, c]) for r, c in zip(*np.where(diff_mask))"
L "                if cur_frame[r, c] == bg and prev_frame[r, c] != bg"
L "            }"
L "            moving = appeared & disappeared or appeared"
L "            if moving:"
L "                mc = min(moving)"
L "                if self.player_color is None:"
L "                    self.player_color = mc"
L "                pos = detect_player_pos(prev_frame, cur_frame)"
L "                if pos:"
L "                    self.player_trail.append(pos)"
L ""
L "        if action == 6 and data:"
L "            x, y = int(data.get('x', 32)), int(data.get('y', 32))"
L "            r, c = min(y, prev_frame.shape[0] - 1), min(x, prev_frame.shape[1] - 1)"
L "            cb, ca = int(prev_frame[r, c]), int(cur_frame[r, c])"
L "            key = (x, y)"
L "            self.toggle_map.setdefault(key, []).append(ca)"
L "            is_toggle = len(set(self.toggle_map[key])) >= 2"
L "            self.click_effs.append({"
L "                'x': x, 'y': y, 'before': cb, 'after': ca,"
L "                'changed': changed, 'diff': diff_count, 'toggle': is_toggle, 'step': step,"
L "            })"
L ""
L "    def get_state(self, last_frame):"
L "        bg = bg_color(last_frame)"
L "        pc = self.player_color"
L "        player = self.player_trail[-1] if self.player_trail else None"
L ""
L "        color_counts = {}"
L "        for color in np.unique(last_frame):"
L "            if color == bg or (pc is not None and color == pc):"
L "                continue"
L "            cnt = int(np.sum(last_frame == color))"
L "            if 1 <= cnt <= 100:"
L "                color_counts[color] = cnt"
L ""
L "        targets = []"
L "        if color_counts:"
L "            goal_color = min(color_counts, key=color_counts.get)"
L "            targets = [tuple(p) for p in np.argwhere(last_frame == goal_color)]"
L "            for c, cnt in sorted(color_counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1]):"
L "                if c == goal_color:"
L "                    continue"
L "                if len(targets) >= 25:"
L "                    break"
L "                targets.extend([tuple(p) for p in np.argwhere(last_frame == c)])"
L ""
L "        walls = np.zeros((64, 64), dtype=bool)"
L "        return player, targets, walls"
L ""
L "class NavigateSpecialist:"
L "    def __init__(self, cls=None):"
L "        self._stuck = 0"
L "        self._prev_pos = None"
L "        self._dir_cycle = 0"
L ""
T GAM
L "        moves = [a for a in avail if a in [1, 2, 3, 4]]"
L "        if not moves:"
L "            return avail[0], None"
L "        if not player:"
L "            act = moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)]"
L "            self._dir_cycle += 1"
L "            return act, None"
L "        if not targets:"
L "            act = moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)]"
L "            self._dir_cycle += 1"
L "            return act, None"
L "        if self._prev_pos == player:"
L "            self._stuck += 1"
L "        else:"
L "            self._stuck = 0"
L "        self._prev_pos = player"
L "        if self._stuck > 8:"
L "            self._stuck = 0"
L "            self._dir_cycle += 1"
L "            return moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)], None"
L ""
L "        near = min(targets, key=lambda t: abs(t[0] - player[0]) + abs(t[1] - player[1]))"
L "        dist = abs(near[0] - player[0]) + abs(near[1] - player[1])"
L "        path = bfs_path(frame, player, tuple(near), walls)"
L "        if path:"
L "            return path[0], None"
L "        if dist <= 2 and 6 in avail:"
L "            return 6, {'x': int(near[1]), 'y': int(near[0])}"
L "        dr = near[0] - player[0]"
L "        dc = near[1] - player[1]"
L "        preferred = 2 if abs(dr) >= abs(dc) and dr > 0 else \\"
L "                    1 if abs(dr) >= abs(dc) else \\"
L "                    4 if dc > 0 else 3"
L "        if preferred in moves:"
L "            return preferred, None"
L "        return moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)], None"

L "def _kaggle_dataset_mounts(inp: Path) -> list[Path]:"
L "    '''All dataset mount dirs under /kaggle/input."

L "    Kaggle uses several nesting conventions:"
L "      <slug>/                          — standard notebook input"

L "            for user_d in d.iterdir():"
L "                if user_d.is_dir():"
L "                    mounts.extend(sd for sd in user_d.iterdir() if sd.is_dir())"

L "            mounts.extend(sd for sd in d.iterdir() if sd.is_dir())"
L "        else:"
L "            mounts.append(d)"

L "                return []"
L ""
L "            goal_val = self.goal_value"
L ""
T GKC
L "            if isinstance(goal_val, (list, tuple, set)):"
L "                if current_val in goal_val:"
L "                    return []"
L "            elif current_val == goal_val:"
L "                return []"
L ""
T GGS
T GIM
L "            best_aid = None"
L "            best_closeness = float('inf')"
L ""
T GAU
L "                d = deltas.get(self.goal_field, None)"
L "                if d is None: continue"
L ""
L "                if isinstance(d, float):"
L "                    # Numeric delta"
L "                    new_val = current_val + d"
T GAV
L "                        if new_val in goal_val:"
L "                            # Hits target in 1 step — ideal"
T GAS
T GMM
T GHW
L "                    else:"
L "                        closeness = abs(new_val - goal_val)"
L "                    if closeness < best_closeness:"
T GQQ
L "                else:"
T GRT
T GAV
L "                        if d in goal_val:"
T GAS
L ""
L "            if best_aid is None:"
L "                return []"
L ""
T GLM
T GIN
L "            if isinstance(d, float) and d != 0:"
L "                if isinstance(goal_val, (list, tuple, set)):"
L "                    # Aim for closest target value"
T GGE
L "                else:"
L "                    target = goal_val"
T GGT
L "            else:"
L "                n_steps = 10  # non-numeric: just try 10 times"
L ""
L "            n_steps = min(n_steps, 300)"
L "            plan = [(best_aid, None)] * n_steps"
L "            self._log.info("
T GPS
L "                f\"(current={current_val} target={goal_val})\")"
L "            return plan"
L ""
T GAC
T GNO
L "        return []"
L ""
L "    def _read_nested(self, obj, path):"
T GHF
L "        try:"
L "            import re"
L "            parts = re.split(r'\\.', path)"
L "            current = obj"
L "            gsbn = getattr(obj, 'get_sprites_by_name', None)"
L "            for part in parts:"
L "                m = re.match(r'^(\\w+)\\[(\\d+)\\]$', part)"
L "                if m:"
L "                    name, idx = m.group(1), int(m.group(2))"
T GEQ
L "                    if callable(gsbn):"
L "                        try:"
L "                            sprites = gsbn(name)"
L "                            if sprites and idx < len(sprites):"
L "                                current = sprites[idx]"
L "                                continue"
T GAX
L "                            pass"
L "                    current = getattr(current, name)[idx]"
L "                else:"
L "                    current = getattr(current, part)"
T GEF
T GEK
L "            return None"

T GAA
L "# CNN FALLBACK (ForgeNet)"
T GRU
L "# Trained online using rewards shaped by WorldModel priors."
T GAA
L "class ForgeNet(nn.Module):"
L "    def __init__(s, in_ch=26, g=64):"
L "        super().__init__()"
L "        s.c1 = nn.Conv2d(in_ch, 32, 3, padding=1)"
L "        s.c2 = nn.Conv2d(32, 64, 3, padding=1)"
L "        s.c3 = nn.Conv2d(64, 128, 3, padding=1)"
L "        s.ap = nn.MaxPool2d(4, 4)"
L "        s.af = nn.Linear(128 * 16 * 16, 256)"
L "        s.ah = nn.Linear(256, 5)"
L "        s.cc1 = nn.Conv2d(128, 64, 3, padding=1)"
L "        s.cc2 = nn.Conv2d(64, 1, 1)"
L ""
L "    def forward(s, x):"
T GNP
L "        af = s.ap(f).reshape(f.size(0), -1)"
L "        al = s.ah(F.relu(s.af(af)))"
T GOP
L "        return torch.cat([al, cl], 1)"
L ""

T GAA
L "# MAIN AGENT"
T GAA
L ""
T GAA
L "# SELECTIVE VISUAL FALLBACK HELPERS"
L "# Grafted from llm-visual-analyzer-arc-agi-3.ipynb, stripped of LLM/runtime glue"
T GAA
L ""
L "def to_2d(obs_or_array):"
L "    if obs_or_array is None:"
L "        return np.zeros((64, 64), dtype=np.int32)"
L "    if hasattr(obs_or_array, 'frame'):"
L "        f = np.array(obs_or_array.frame, dtype=np.int32)"
L "    else:"
L "        f = np.asarray(obs_or_array, dtype=np.int32)"
L "    if f.ndim == 3:"
L "        f = f[-1]"
L "    if f.ndim != 2:"
L "        f = np.zeros((64, 64), dtype=np.int32)"
L "    return f"
L ""
L "def bg_color(frame):"
L "    return int(np.bincount(np.asarray(frame).flatten()).argmax())"
L ""
L "def bfs_path(grid, start, goal, walls):"
L "    if start == goal:"
L "        return []"
L "    h, w = grid.shape"
L "    q, vis = deque([(start, [])]), {start}"
L "    deltas = {1:(-1,0), 2:(1,0), 3:(0,-1), 4:(0,1)}"
L "    while q:"
L "        pos, path = q.popleft()"
L "        for act, (dr, dc) in deltas.items():"
L "            n = (pos[0] + dr, pos[1] + dc)"
L "            if 0 <= n[0] < h and 0 <= n[1] < w and n not in vis and not walls[n[0], n[1]]:"
L "                vis.add(n)"
L "                new_path = path + [act]"
L "                if n == goal:"
L "                    return new_path"
L "                q.append((n, new_path))"
L "    return []"
L ""
L "def lawnmower_scan(w=64, h=64, step=8):"
L "    pos = []"
L "    for i, y in enumerate(range(0, h, step)):"
L "        xs = range(0, w, step) if i % 2 == 0 else range(w - 1, -1, -step)"
L "        for x in xs:"
L "            pos.append((x, y))"
L "    return pos"
L ""
L "def gf2_solve(toggle_matrix, target_state):"
L "    n, m = len(target_state), len(toggle_matrix)"
L "    if m == 0 or n == 0:"
L "        return []"
L "    aug = np.zeros((n, m + 1), dtype=int)"
L "    for i in range(n):"
L "        for j in range(m):"
L "            aug[i, j] = int(toggle_matrix[j][i]) % 2"
L "        aug[i, m] = int(target_state[i]) % 2"
L "    pivot_cols, row = [], 0"
L "    for col in range(m):"
L "        found = next((r for r in range(row, n) if aug[r, col] == 1), -1)"
L "        if found == -1:"
L "            continue"
L "        aug[[row, found]] = aug[[found, row]]"
L "        for r in range(n):"
L "            if r != row and aug[r, col] == 1:"
L "                aug[r] = (aug[r] + aug[row]) % 2"
L "        pivot_cols.append(col)"
L "        row += 1"
L "    sol = np.zeros(m, dtype=int)"
L "    for i, col in enumerate(pivot_cols):"
L "        if i < n:"
L "            sol[col] = aug[i, m]"
L "    return [j for j in range(m) if sol[j] == 1]"
L ""
L "def detect_player_pos(prev_frame, cur_frame):"
L "    diff = (prev_frame != cur_frame)"
L "    if not diff.any():"
L "        return None"
L "    bg = bg_color(prev_frame)"
L "    rows, cols = np.where(diff)"
L "    for r, c in zip(rows, cols):"
L "        if prev_frame[r, c] == bg and cur_frame[r, c] != bg:"
L "            return (int(r), int(c))"
L "    return (int(rows.mean()), int(cols.mean()))"
L ""
L "class GameObserver:"
L "    def __init__(self):"
L "        self.jsonl = []"
L "        self.action_eff = defaultdict(list)"
L "        self.click_effs = []"
L "        self.toggle_map = {}"
L "        self.player_trail = []"
L "        self.player_color = None"
L "        self._bg = None"
L ""
L "    def start_phase(self, frame, avail):"
L "        self._bg = bg_color(frame)"
L ""
L "    def record(self, prev_frame, action, data, cur_frame, step, obs2=None):"
L "        changed = not np.array_equal(prev_frame, cur_frame)"
L "        diff_count = int(np.sum(prev_frame != cur_frame))"
L "        bg = self._bg if self._bg is not None else bg_color(prev_frame)"
L "        self.action_eff[action].append({'changed': changed, 'diff': diff_count})"
L ""
L "        if action in [1, 2, 3, 4] and changed:"
L "            diff_mask = (prev_frame != cur_frame)"
L "            appeared = {"
L "                int(cur_frame[r, c]) for r, c in zip(*np.where(diff_mask))"
L "                if prev_frame[r, c] == bg and cur_frame[r, c] != bg"
L "            }"
L "            disappeared = {"
L "                int(prev_frame[r, c]) for r, c in zip(*np.where(diff_mask))"
L "                if cur_frame[r, c] == bg and prev_frame[r, c] != bg"
L "            }"
L "            moving = appeared & disappeared or appeared"
L "            if moving:"
L "                mc = min(moving)"
L "                if self.player_color is None:"
L "                    self.player_color = mc"
L "                pos = detect_player_pos(prev_frame, cur_frame)"
L "                if pos:"
L "                    self.player_trail.append(pos)"
L ""
L "        if action == 6 and data:"
L "            x, y = int(data.get('x', 32)), int(data.get('y', 32))"
L "            r, c = min(y, prev_frame.shape[0] - 1), min(x, prev_frame.shape[1] - 1)"
L "            cb, ca = int(prev_frame[r, c]), int(cur_frame[r, c])"
L "            key = (x, y)"
L "            self.toggle_map.setdefault(key, []).append(ca)"
L "            is_toggle = len(set(self.toggle_map[key])) >= 2"
L "            self.click_effs.append({"
L "                'x': x, 'y': y, 'before': cb, 'after': ca,"
L "                'changed': changed, 'diff': diff_count, 'toggle': is_toggle, 'step': step,"
L "            })"
L ""
L "    def get_state(self, last_frame):"
L "        bg = bg_color(last_frame)"
L "        pc = self.player_color"
L "        player = self.player_trail[-1] if self.player_trail else None"
L ""
L "        color_counts = {}"
L "        for color in np.unique(last_frame):"
L "            if color == bg or (pc is not None and color == pc):"
L "                continue"
L "            cnt = int(np.sum(last_frame == color))"
L "            if 1 <= cnt <= 100:"
L "                color_counts[color] = cnt"
L ""
L "        targets = []"
L "        if color_counts:"
L "            goal_color = min(color_counts, key=color_counts.get)"
L "            targets = [tuple(p) for p in np.argwhere(last_frame == goal_color)]"
L "            for c, cnt in sorted(color_counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1]):"
L "                if c == goal_color:"
L "                    continue"
L "                if len(targets) >= 25:"
L "                    break"
L "                targets.extend([tuple(p) for p in np.argwhere(last_frame == c)])"
L ""
L "        walls = np.zeros((64, 64), dtype=bool)"
L "        return player, targets, walls"
L ""
L "class NavigateSpecialist:"
L "    def __init__(self, cls=None):"
L "        self._stuck = 0"
L "        self._prev_pos = None"
L "        self._dir_cycle = 0"
L ""
T GAM
L "        moves = [a for a in avail if a in [1, 2, 3, 4]]"
L "        if not moves:"
L "            return avail[0], None"
L "        if not player:"
L "            act = moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)]"
L "            self._dir_cycle += 1"
L "            return act, None"
L "        if not targets:"
L "            act = moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)]"
L "            self._dir_cycle += 1"
L "            return act, None"
L "        if self._prev_pos == player:"
L "            self._stuck += 1"
L "        else:"
L "            self._stuck = 0"
L "        self._prev_pos = player"
L "        if self._stuck > 8:"
L "            self._stuck = 0"
L "            self._dir_cycle += 1"
L "            return moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)], None"
L ""
L "        near = min(targets, key=lambda t: abs(t[0] - player[0]) + abs(t[1] - player[1]))"
L "        dist = abs(near[0] - player[0]) + abs(near[1] - player[1])"
L "        path = bfs_path(frame, player, tuple(near), walls)"
L "        if path:"
L "            return path[0], None"
L "        if dist <= 2 and 6 in avail:"
L "            return 6, {'x': int(near[1]), 'y': int(near[0])}"
L "        dr = near[0] - player[0]"
L "        dc = near[1] - player[1]"
L "        preferred = 2 if abs(dr) >= abs(dc) and dr > 0 else \\"
L "                    1 if abs(dr) >= abs(dc) else \\"
L "                    4 if dc > 0 else 3"
L "        if preferred in moves:"
L "            return preferred, None"
L "        return moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)], None"
L ""
L "class LawnmowerSpecialist:"
L "    def __init__(self, cls=None, step=8):"

T GEJ

L "            x, y = unclicked[0]"
L "            self._clicked.add((x, y))"
L "            return 6, {'x': x, 'y': y}"
L "        while self._idx < len(self.positions):"
L "            x, y = self.positions[self._idx]"
L "            self._idx += 1"
L "            if (x, y) not in self._clicked:"
L "                self._clicked.add((x, y))"
L "                return 6, {'x': x, 'y': y}"

L "        x, y = self.positions[turn % len(self.positions)]"
L "        return 6, {'x': x, 'y': y}"

T GAD
L "def _kaggle_dataset_mounts(inp: Path) -> list[Path]:"
L "    '''All dataset mount dirs under /kaggle/input."
L ""
L "    Kaggle uses several nesting conventions:"
L "      <slug>/                          — standard notebook input"
L "      datasets/<user>/<slug>/          — API-added datasets"
L "      competitions/<competition>/      — competition data"
L "    '''"
L "    mounts: list[Path] = []"
L "    for d in inp.iterdir():"
L "        if not d.is_dir():"
L "            continue"
L "        if d.name == \"datasets\":"
L "            for user_d in d.iterdir():"
L "                if user_d.is_dir():"
L "                    mounts.extend(sd for sd in user_d.iterdir() if sd.is_dir())"
L "        elif d.name == \"competitions\":"
L "            mounts.extend(sd for sd in d.iterdir() if sd.is_dir())"
L "        else:"
L "            mounts.append(d)"

L "# ==================== BFS SOLVER ===================="
L ""
L "class BFSSolver:"
L "    \"\"\"Offline BFS solver using direct game class instantiation.\"\"\""
L ""
L "    def __init__(self, game_path, game_class_name, scan_timeout=3, bfs_timeout=120):"
L "        self.game_path = game_path"
L "        self.class_name = game_class_name"
L "        self.scan_timeout = scan_timeout"
L "        self.bfs_timeout = bfs_timeout"
L "        self.game_cls = None"
L "        self.solutions = {}  # level_idx → action list"
L ""
L "    def load(self):"
L "        \"\"\"Load the game class from source.\"\"\""
L "        try:"
T GBO
L "            mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)"
L "            spec.loader.exec_module(mod)"
T GSU
L "            return True"
T GAC
L "            logger.warning(f\"BFS: Failed to load game class: {e}\")"
L "            return False"
L ""
L "    def _state_hash(self, g, frame, hidden_fields=None):"
L "        \"\"\"v10: Hash frame + discovered hidden scalar fields (fast).\"\"\""
L "        fh = hashlib.md5(frame.tobytes()).hexdigest()[:16]"
L "        if hidden_fields:"
L "            # Append hidden field values to hash — much faster than pickle(__dict__)"
L "            extras = []"
L "            for field_name in hidden_fields:"
L "                try:"
L "                    v = getattr(g, field_name, None)"
L "                    if v is not None:"
L "                        extras.append(f\"{field_name}={v}\")"
L "                except:"
L "                    pass"
L "            if extras:"
L "                return fh + \"|\" + \"|\".join(extras)"
L "        return fh"
L ""
L "    def _probe_hidden_fields(self, game, actions):"
L "        \"\"\"v10: Dynamic state probing — discover which scalar fields change per action."
L "        Returns list of field names that are hidden state (change without pixel change).\"\"\""
L "        if not actions:"
L "            return []"
L "        # Get initial scalar snapshot"
L "        initial = {}"
T GCN
L "            if isinstance(v, (int, float, bool)) and not k.startswith('__'):"
L "                initial[k] = v"
L ""
L "        # Try each action, see what scalars change"
L "        changing_fields = set()"
L "        frame0 = game.get_pixels(0, 0, 64, 64)"
L "        for act_id, data in actions[:10]:  # probe first 10 actions"
L "            g = copy.deepcopy(game)"
L "            try:"
L "                ai = ActionInput(id=GameAction.from_id(act_id), data=data) if data else ActionInput(id=GameAction.from_id(act_id))"
L "                g.perform_action(ai, raw=True)"
L "            except:"
L "                continue"
L "            f = g.get_pixels(0, 0, 64, 64)"
L "            pixels_changed = np.sum(frame0 != f) > 0"
L "            for k, v in g.__dict__.items():"
L "                if isinstance(v, (int, float, bool)) and not k.startswith('__'):"
L "                    if k in initial and v != initial[k]:"
L "                        # Field changed — is it hidden? (not reflected in pixels)"
L "                        if k not in ('_action_count', '_full_reset', '_action_complete'):"
L "                            changing_fields.add(k)"
L ""
L "        # Filter: only keep fields that change WITHOUT pixel changes (truly hidden)"
L "        # Also keep counters that might be win-relevant"
L "        hidden = []"
L "        for f in changing_fields:"
L "            if f.startswith('_') and f not in ('_current_level_index', '_score'):"
L "                continue"
L "            hidden.append(f)"
L "        return sorted(hidden)"
L ""
L "    def _scan_actions(self, game, f0, bg):"
L "        \"\"\"Scan for effective actions. Returns list of (action_id, data).\"\"\""
L "        avail = game._available_actions"
L "        actions = []"
L "        # Directional/interact actions"
L "        for a in [a for a in avail if a <= 5]:"
L "            g = copy.deepcopy(game)"
L "            try:"
L "                r = g.perform_action(ActionInput(id=GameAction.from_id(a)), raw=True)"
L "                if r.frame and np.sum(f0 != np.array(r.frame[-1])) > 0:"
L "                    actions.append((a, None))"
L "            except:"
L "                pass"
L "        # Click actions (proven v9 scan — don't change this)"
L "        if 6 in avail:"
L "            t0 = time.time()"
L "            seen_effects = set()"
L "            for y in range(0, 64, 2):"
L "                if time.time() - t0 > self.scan_timeout:"
L "                    break"
L "                for x in range(0, 64, 2):"
L "                    if f0[y, x] == bg:"
L "                        continue"
L "                    g = copy.deepcopy(game)"
L "                    try:"
L "                        r = g.perform_action("
L "                            ActionInput(id=GameAction.ACTION6, data={'x': x, 'y': y, 'game_id': 'bfs'}),"
L "                            raw=True"
L "                        )"
L "                        if not r.frame:"
L "                            continue"
L "                        f = np.array(r.frame[-1])"
L "                        diff = np.sum(f0 != f)"
L "                        if diff > 0:"
L "                            # v9: compress equivalent clicks (same effect = same action)"
L "                            effect_hash = hashlib.md5(f.tobytes()).hexdigest()[:12]"
L "                            if effect_hash not in seen_effects:"
L "                                seen_effects.add(effect_hash)"
L "                                actions.append((6, {'x': x, 'y': y, 'game_id': 'bfs'}))"
L "                    except:"
L "                        pass"
L "        return actions"
L ""
L "    def solve_level(self, level_idx, max_states=500000, prev_solution=None):"
L "        \"\"\"Find optimal solution for a level via BFS.\"\"\""
L "        if not self.game_cls:"
L "            return None"
L ""
L "        game = self.game_cls()"
L "        game.set_level(level_idx)"
L "        game.perform_action(ActionInput(id=GameAction.RESET), raw=True)"
L ""
L "        r0 = game.perform_action(ActionInput(id=GameAction.RESET), raw=True)"
L "        if not r0.frame:"
L "            return None"
L "        f0 = np.array(r0.frame[-1])"
L "        bg = int(np.bincount(f0.flatten(), minlength=16).argmax())"
L ""
L "        # v9: Try solution transfer from previous level first"
L "        if prev_solution and level_idx > 0:"
L "            transfer_result = self._try_transfer(game, level_idx, prev_solution, f0)"
L "            if transfer_result:"
L "                return transfer_result"
L ""
L "        # Phase 1: Scan for effective actions"
L "        actions = self._scan_actions(game, f0, bg)"
L ""
L "        # v18: Warm-up unlock for locked initial states (sc25-type)"
L "        if not actions:"
L "            avail = game._available_actions"
L "            for warmup_id in [a for a in avail if a <= 4]:"
L "                g_warmup = copy.deepcopy(game)"
L "                try:"
L "                    g_warmup.perform_action(ActionInput(id=GameAction.from_id(warmup_id)), raw=True)"
L "                    f_after = np.array(g_warmup.get_pixels(0, 0, 64, 64))"
L "                    warmup_actions = self._scan_actions(g_warmup, f_after, bg)"
L "                    if warmup_actions:"
L "                        logger.info(f\"BFS L{level_idx}: UNLOCKED with ACTION{warmup_id}! {len(warmup_actions)} actions\")"
L "                        game = g_warmup; f0 = f_after; actions = warmup_actions"
L "                        break"
L "                except: pass"
L ""
L "        logger.info(f\"BFS L{level_idx}: {len(actions)} effective actions\")"
L "        if not actions:"
L "            return None"
L ""
L "        # Phase 2: BFS — first try with frame hash (fast, proven for 12/25)"
L "        hidden_fields = None  # start without hidden fields"
L "        visited = set()"
L "        queue = deque()"
L "        h0 = self._state_hash(game, f0, None)"
L "        visited.add(h0)"
L "        queue.append((copy.deepcopy(game), [], 0))"
L ""
L "        t0 = time.time()"
L "        explored = 0"
L ""
L "        while queue and explored < max_states and (time.time() - t0) < self.bfs_timeout:"
L "            g, hist, depth = queue.popleft()"
L ""
L "            for act_id, data in actions:"
L "                g2 = copy.deepcopy(g)"
L "                try:"
L "                    ai = ActionInput(id=GameAction.from_id(act_id), data=data) if data else ActionInput(id=GameAction.from_id(act_id))"
L "                    r = g2.perform_action(ai, raw=True)"
L "                except:"
L "                    continue"
L "                explored += 1"
L ""
L "                if not r.frame:"
L "                    continue"
L "                f = np.array(r.frame[-1])"
L "                h = self._state_hash(g2, f, hidden_fields if hidden_fields else None)"
L "                if h in visited:"
L "                    continue"
L "                visited.add(h)"
L ""
L "                new_hist = hist + [(act_id, data)]"
L ""
L "                # Win detection"
T GBH
L "                    elapsed = time.time() - t0"
L "                    logger.info(f\"BFS L{level_idx}: SOLVED in {len(new_hist)} actions ({explored} explored, {elapsed:.1f}s)\")"
L "                    self.solutions[level_idx] = new_hist"
L "                    return new_hist"
L ""
L "                if depth < 30:"
L "                    queue.append((g2, new_hist, depth + 1))"
L ""
L "        elapsed_first = time.time() - t0"
L "        logger.info(f\"BFS L{level_idx}: first pass timeout ({explored} explored, {len(visited)} unique, {elapsed_first:.1f}s)\")"
L ""
L "        # v10: If too few unique states found → hidden state detected → retry with probed fields"
L "        if len(visited) < 50 and elapsed_first < self.bfs_timeout * 0.8:"
L "            hidden_fields = self._probe_hidden_fields(game, actions)"
L "            if hidden_fields:"
L "                logger.info(f\"BFS L{level_idx}: RETRY with hidden fields: {hidden_fields}\")"
L "                visited2 = set()"
L "                queue2 = deque()"
L "                game2 = self.game_cls()"
L "                game2.set_level(level_idx)"
L "                game2.perform_action(ActionInput(id=GameAction.RESET), raw=True)"
L "                game2.perform_action(ActionInput(id=GameAction.RESET), raw=True)"
L "                f0_2 = np.array(game2.perform_action(ActionInput(id=GameAction.RESET), raw=True).frame[-1])"
L "                h0_2 = self._state_hash(game2, f0_2, hidden_fields)"
L "                visited2.add(h0_2)"
L "                queue2.append((copy.deepcopy(game2), [], 0))"
L "                t0_2 = time.time()"
L "                explored2 = 0"
L "                remaining = max(30, self.bfs_timeout - elapsed_first)"
L "                while queue2 and explored2 < max_states and (time.time() - t0_2) < remaining:"
L "                    g, hist, depth = queue2.popleft()"
L "                    for act_id, data in actions:"
L "                        g2 = copy.deepcopy(g)"
L "                        try:"
L "                            ai = ActionInput(id=GameAction.from_id(act_id), data=data) if data else ActionInput(id=GameAction.from_id(act_id))"
L "                            r = g2.perform_action(ai, raw=True)"
L "                        except: continue"
L "                        explored2 += 1"
L "                        if not r.frame: continue"
L "                        f = np.array(r.frame[-1])"
L "                        h = self._state_hash(g2, f, hidden_fields)"
L "                        if h in visited2: continue"
L "                        visited2.add(h)"
L "                        new_hist = hist + [(act_id, data)]"
L "                        if r.levels_completed > level_idx or g2._current_level_index > level_idx:"
L "                            logger.info(f\"BFS L{level_idx}: SOLVED (hidden retry) in {len(new_hist)} actions ({explored2} explored)\")"
L "                            self.solutions[level_idx] = new_hist"
L "                            return new_hist"
L "                        if depth < 30:"
L "                            queue2.append((g2, new_hist, depth + 1))"
L "                logger.info(f\"BFS L{level_idx}: hidden retry also failed ({explored2} explored, {len(visited2)} unique)\")"
L "        return None"
L ""
L "    def _try_transfer(self, game, level_idx, prev_solution, f1):"
L "        \"\"\"v9: Transfer previous level's solution to current level.\"\"\""
L "        try:"
L "            # Try executing prev solution directly (sometimes levels share exact solution)"
L "            g = copy.deepcopy(game)"
L "            for i, (act_id, data) in enumerate(prev_solution):"
L "                try:"
L "                    ai = ActionInput(id=GameAction.from_id(act_id), data=data) if data else ActionInput(id=GameAction.from_id(act_id))"
L "                    r = g.perform_action(ai, raw=True)"
L "                    if r.levels_completed > level_idx or g._current_level_index > level_idx:"
L "                        logger.info(f\"BFS L{level_idx}: TRANSFER SUCCESS (direct replay, {i+1} actions)\")"
L "                        sol = prev_solution[:i+1]"
L "                        self.solutions[level_idx] = sol"
L "                        return sol"
L "                except:"
L "                    break"
L ""
L "            # Try object-relative transfer (CHRONOS Opus T11)"
L "            prev_game = self.game_cls()"
L "            prev_game.set_level(level_idx - 1)"
L "            prev_game.perform_action(ActionInput(id=GameAction.RESET), raw=True)"
L "            r_prev = prev_game.perform_action(ActionInput(id=GameAction.RESET), raw=True)"
L "            if not r_prev.frame:"
L "                return None"
L "            f0 = np.array(r_prev.frame[-1])"
L "            bg = int(np.bincount(f0.flatten(), minlength=16).argmax())"
L ""
L "            # Extract objects from both levels"
L "            def get_objects(frame, bg_c):"
L "                objs = []"
L "                for c in range(16):"
L "                    if c == bg_c:"
L "                        continue"
L "                    mask = (frame == c)"
L "                    npix = int(np.sum(mask))"
L "                    if npix < 2:"
L "                        continue"
L "                    ys, xs = np.where(mask)"
L "                    objs.append({'color': c, 'cx': float(np.mean(xs)), 'cy': float(np.mean(ys)), 'n': npix})"
L "                return sorted(objs, key=lambda o: (o['color'], -o['n']))"
L ""
L "            objs_prev = get_objects(f0, bg)"
L "            objs_curr = get_objects(f1, bg)"
L ""
L "            if not objs_prev or not objs_curr:"
L "                return None"
L ""
L "            # Match objects by color + relative size"
L "            matched = []"
L "            for op in objs_prev:"
L "                best = None"
L "                best_dist = float('inf')"
L "                for oc in objs_curr:"
L "                    if oc['color'] == op['color'] and abs(oc['n'] - op['n']) < max(op['n'], oc['n']) * 0.5:"
L "                        d = abs(oc['cx'] - op['cx']) + abs(oc['cy'] - op['cy'])"
L "                        if d < best_dist:"
L "                            best_dist = d"
L "                            best = oc"
L "                if best:"
L "                    matched.append((op, best))"
L ""
L "            if not matched:"
L "                return None"
L ""
L "            # Compute offset"
L "            dx = np.mean([m[1]['cx'] - m[0]['cx'] for m in matched])"
L "            dy = np.mean([m[1]['cy'] - m[0]['cy'] for m in matched])"
L ""
L "            # Apply offset to click actions"
L "            transferred = []"
L "            for act_id, data in prev_solution:"
L "                if data and 'x' in data:"
L "                    new_data = dict(data)"
L "                    new_data['x'] = max(0, min(63, int(data['x'] + dx)))"
L "                    new_data['y'] = max(0, min(63, int(data['y'] + dy)))"
L "                    transferred.append((act_id, new_data))"
L "                else:"
L "                    transferred.append((act_id, data))"
L ""
L "            # Validate transferred solution"
L "            g = copy.deepcopy(game)"
L "            for i, (act_id, data) in enumerate(transferred):"
L "                try:"
L "                    ai = ActionInput(id=GameAction.from_id(act_id), data=data) if data else ActionInput(id=GameAction.from_id(act_id))"
L "                    r = g.perform_action(ai, raw=True)"
L "                    if r.levels_completed > level_idx or g._current_level_index > level_idx:"
L "                        logger.info(f\"BFS L{level_idx}: TRANSFER SUCCESS (offset dx={dx:.0f},dy={dy:.0f}, {i+1} actions)\")"
L "                        sol = transferred[:i+1]"
L "                        self.solutions[level_idx] = sol"
L "                        return sol"
L "                except:"
L "                    break"
L ""
T GAC
L "            logger.warning(f\"BFS transfer failed: {e}\")"
L "        return None"
L ""
L ""
L "def find_game_source_and_class(game_id, arc_env=None):"
L "    \"\"\"Find the game .py file and class name.\"\"\""
L "    gid = game_id.split('-')[0]"
L "    cls_name = gid.capitalize()"
L "    if len(gid) == 4 and gid[0].isalpha():"
L "        cls_name = gid[0].upper() + gid[1:]"
L ""
L "    src = None"
L "    # Method 1: from arc_env"
L "    if arc_env and hasattr(arc_env, 'environment_info'):"
L "        ei = arc_env.environment_info"
L "        if hasattr(ei, 'local_dir') and ei.local_dir:"
L "            from pathlib import Path"
L "            ld = Path(ei.local_dir)"
L "            for candidate in [ld / f\"{gid}.py\", ld / f\"{cls_name.lower()}.py\"]:"
L "                if candidate.exists():"
L "                    src = str(candidate)"
L "                    # Get class name from source"
L "                    import re"
L "                    content = candidate.read_text()[:2000]"
L "                    m = re.search(r'class\\s+(\\w+)\\s*\\(\\s*ARCBaseGame', content)"
L "                    if m:"
L "                        cls_name = m.group(1)"
L "                    break"
L ""
L "    # Method 2: glob"
L "    if not src:"
L "        for pattern in ["
L "            f\"/tmp/*/{gid}/*/{gid}.py\","
L "            f\"/kaggle/*/{gid}*/{gid}.py\","
L "            f\"**/game_sources/**/{gid}.py\","
L "        ]:"
L "            matches = glob.glob(pattern, recursive=True)"
L "            if matches:"
L "                src = matches[0]"
L "                import re"
L "                content = open(src).read()[:2000]"
L "                m = re.search(r'class\\s+(\\w+)\\s*\\(\\s*ARCBaseGame', content)"
L "                if m:"
L "                    cls_name = m.group(1)"
L "                break"
L ""
L "    return src, cls_name"
L ""
L ""
L "# ==================== CNN FALLBACK (v8 core) ===================="
L ""
L "class CBAM(nn.Module):"
L "    def __init__(s, ch, r=16):"
L "        super().__init__()"
L "        s.fc1=nn.Linear(ch,max(ch//r,4)); s.fc2=nn.Linear(max(ch//r,4),ch)"
L "        s.sp=nn.Conv2d(2,1,7,padding=3)"
L "    def forward(s, x):"
L "        B,C,H,W=x.shape"
L "        w=torch.sigmoid(s.fc2(F.relu(s.fc1(x.mean(dim=[2,3]))))); x=x*w.view(B,C,1,1)"
L "        a=torch.sigmoid(s.sp(torch.cat([x.max(1,keepdim=True)[0],x.mean(1,keepdim=True)],1)))"
L "        return x*a"
L ""
L "class ActionEffectAttention(nn.Module):"
L "    def __init__(s, feat_dim=64, mem_dim=32, n_actions=5):"
L "        super().__init__()"
L "        s.mem_dim=mem_dim"
L "        s.diff_enc=nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(1,8,8,stride=8),nn.ReLU(),nn.Conv2d(8,16,4,stride=4),nn.ReLU(),nn.Flatten(),nn.Linear(16*2*2,mem_dim))"
L "        s.q_proj=nn.Linear(feat_dim,mem_dim)"
L "        s.v_proj=nn.Linear(mem_dim+1+n_actions,n_actions)"
L "        s.scale=mem_dim**0.5"
L "    def forward(s, cnn_feat, mem_diffs, mem_actions, mem_rewards):"
L "        B,M=mem_actions.shape"
L "        if M==0:return torch.zeros(B,5,device=cnn_feat.device)"
L "        keys=s.diff_enc(mem_diffs.reshape(B*M,1,64,64)).reshape(B,M,s.mem_dim)"
L "        q=s.q_proj(cnn_feat).unsqueeze(1)"
L "        attn=F.softmax(torch.bmm(q,keys.transpose(1,2))/s.scale,dim=-1)"
L "        act_oh=F.one_hot(mem_actions.clamp(0,4),5).float()"
L "        vals=torch.cat([keys,mem_rewards.unsqueeze(-1),act_oh],dim=-1)"
L "        ctx=torch.bmm(attn,vals).squeeze(1)"
L "        return s.v_proj(ctx)"
L ""
L "class ForgeNet(nn.Module):"
L "    def __init__(s, in_ch=26, g=64):"
L "        super().__init__()"
L "        s.g=g"
L "        s.c1=nn.Conv2d(in_ch,32,3,padding=1);s.c2=nn.Conv2d(32,64,3,padding=1)"
L "        s.c3=nn.Conv2d(64,128,3,padding=1);s.c4=nn.Conv2d(128,256,3,padding=1)"
L "        s.attn=CBAM(256);s.ar=nn.Conv2d(256,64,1);s.ap=nn.MaxPool2d(4,4)"
L "        s.af=nn.Linear(64*16*16,256);s.ah=nn.Linear(256,5);s.dr=nn.Dropout(0.15)"
L "        s.cc1=nn.Conv2d(256,128,3,padding=1);s.cc2=nn.Conv2d(128,64,3,padding=1)"
L "        s.cc3=nn.Conv2d(64,32,1);s.cc4=nn.Conv2d(32,1,1)"
L "        s.gp=nn.AdaptiveAvgPool2d(1);s.gf=nn.Linear(256,64)"
L "        s.aea=ActionEffectAttention(feat_dim=64,mem_dim=32,n_actions=5)"
L "    def forward(s, x, mem_diffs=None, mem_actions=None, mem_rewards=None):"
L "        x=F.relu(s.c1(x));x=F.relu(s.c2(x));x=F.relu(s.c3(x));f=F.relu(s.c4(x))"
L "        f=s.attn(f);af=F.relu(s.ar(f));af=s.ap(af).reshape(f.size(0),-1)"
L "        al=s.ah(s.dr(F.relu(s.af(af))))"
L "        cf=F.relu(s.cc1(f));cf=F.relu(s.cc2(cf));cf=F.relu(s.cc3(cf))"
L "        cl=s.cc4(cf).reshape(f.size(0),-1)"
L "        if mem_diffs is not None and mem_actions is not None:"
L "            gf=s.gf(s.gp(f).reshape(f.size(0),-1))"
L "            al=al+s.aea(gf,mem_diffs,mem_actions,mem_rewards)"
L "        return torch.cat([al,cl],1)"
L ""
L ""
L "def fast_objects(frame, bg):"
L "    objs=[]"
L "    for c in range(16):"
L "        if c==bg:continue"
L "        mask=(frame==c);npix=int(np.sum(mask))"
L "        if npix<4 or npix>3000:continue"
L "        ys,xs=np.where(mask)"
L "        objs.append((c,float(np.mean(xs)),float(np.mean(ys)),npix))"
L "    return objs"
L ""
L ""
L "# ==================== AGENT ===================="
L ""
L "class MyAgent(Agent):"
L "    MAX_ACTIONS = float('inf')"
L "    _MAX_FRAMES = 10"
L ""
L "    def __init__(s, *a, **kw):"
L "        super().__init__(*a, **kw)"
L "        seed = int(time.time()*1e6) + hash(s.game_id) % 1000000"
L "        random.seed(seed); np.random.seed(seed%(2**32-1)); torch.manual_seed(seed%(2**32-1))"
L "        s.start_time = time.time()"
L "        s.device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else ('mps' if torch.backends.mps.is_available() else 'cpu'))"
L "        s.G=64; s.IN=26"
L "        s.net=None; s.opt=None"
L "        s.buf=deque(maxlen=50000); s.buf_h=set()"
L "        s.bsz=64; s.tfreq=10"
L "        s.pt=None; s.pai=None; s.pr=None; s.ph=None"
L "        s.cl=-1; s.fhist=deque(maxlen=6); s.la=0"
L "        s.al=[GameAction.ACTION1,GameAction.ACTION2,GameAction.ACTION3,GameAction.ACTION4,GameAction.ACTION5]"
L "        s._wd=False; s._bg=0; s._wm=None"
L "        s._aem_diffs=deque(maxlen=256); s._aem_actions=deque(maxlen=256); s._aem_rewards=deque(maxlen=256)"
L "        s._ckpt_hash=None; s._unproductive=0; s._undo_avail=False"
L "        s._eps=0.15; s._eps_min=0.03; s._eps_decay=0.9997"
L "        s._prev_objs=None; s._obj_moved=0"
L "        # BFS solver"
L "        s._bfs = None"
L "        s._bfs_solution = None  # current level's solution"
L "        s._bfs_step = 0  # current step in solution"
L "        s._bfs_tried = False"
L ""
L "    def append_frame(s, f):"
L "        s.frames.append(f)"
T GCT
L "        if f.guid: s.guid = f.guid"
L "        if hasattr(s, \"recorder\") and not s.is_playback:"
L "            import json; s.recorder.record(json.loads(f.model_dump_json()))"
L ""
L "    def _lvl(s, f): return getattr(f, 'score', None) or f.levels_completed"
L "    def _raw(s, fd): return np.array(fd.frame, dtype=np.int64)[-1]"
L ""
L "    def _init_bfs(s):"
L "        \"\"\"Initialize BFS solver on first call.\"\"\""
L "        src, cls = find_game_source_and_class(s.game_id, s.arc_env)"
L "        if src:"
L "            s._bfs = BFSSolver(src, cls, scan_timeout=5, bfs_timeout=180)"
L "            if s._bfs.load():"
L "                logger.info(f\"BFS: loaded {cls} from {src}\")"
L "            else:"
L "                s._bfs = None"
L "                logger.warning(f\"BFS: failed to load game class\")"
L "        else:"
L "            logger.warning(f\"BFS: game source not found for {s.game_id}\")"
L ""
L "    def _try_bfs_solve(s, level_idx):"
L "        \"\"\"Try to solve current level with BFS, using previous solution for transfer.\"\"\""
L "        if s._bfs is None:"
L "            return None"
L "        prev_sol = s._bfs.solutions.get(level_idx - 1) if level_idx > 0 else None"
L "        sol = s._bfs.solve_level(level_idx, prev_solution=prev_sol)"
L "        if sol:"
L "            s._bfs_solution = sol"
L "            s._bfs_step = 0"
L "            return sol"
L "        return None"
L ""
L "    def _tensor(s, fd):"
L "        frame = s._raw(fd)"
L "        oh=torch.zeros(16,64,64,dtype=torch.float32)"
L "        oh.scatter_(0,torch.from_numpy(frame).unsqueeze(0),1)"
L "        cnt=np.bincount(frame.flatten(),minlength=16)"
L "        s._bg=int(cnt.argmax());mx=max(cnt.max(),1)"
L "        bg_m=(frame==s._bg).astype(np.float32)"
L "        rar=np.zeros((64,64),np.float32)"
L "        for c in range(16):"
L "            if cnt[c]>0:rar[frame==c]=1.0-cnt[c]/mx"
L "        pad=np.pad(frame,1,mode='edge')"
L "        edge=((frame!=pad[:-2,1:-1])|(frame!=pad[2:,1:-1])|(frame!=pad[1:-1,:-2])|(frame!=pad[1:-1,2:])).astype(np.float32)"
L "        rp=np.linspace(0,1,64,dtype=np.float32).reshape(64,1).repeat(64,1)"
L "        cp=np.linspace(0,1,64,dtype=np.float32).reshape(1,64).repeat(64,0)"
L "        aug=torch.from_numpy(np.stack([bg_m,rar,edge,rp,cp]))"
L "        d1=torch.zeros(3,64,64,dtype=torch.float32)"
L "        for i,prev in enumerate(reversed(list(s.fhist))):"
L "            if i>=3:break"
L "            d1[i]=torch.from_numpy((frame!=prev).astype(np.float32))"
L "        d2=torch.zeros(2,64,64,dtype=torch.float32)"
L "        h=list(s.fhist)"
L "        if len(h)>=2:d2[0]=torch.from_numpy((h[-1]!=h[-2]).astype(np.float32))"
L "        if len(h)>=4:d2[1]=torch.from_numpy((h[-2]!=h[-4]).astype(np.float32))"
L "        s.fhist.append(frame.copy())"
L "        return torch.cat([oh,aug,d1,d2],0).to(s.device)"
L ""
L "    def _detect_template(s, frame):"
L "        mask=torch.ones(4096,dtype=torch.float32)"
L "        col_act=np.sum(frame!=s._bg,axis=0)"
L "        for c in range(20,44):"
L "            if col_act[c]<=2 and np.sum(col_act[:c]>0)>=5 and np.sum(col_act[c+1:]>0)>=5:"
L "                for y in range(64):"
L "                    for x in range(c+1):mask[y*64+x]=0.05"
L "                return mask"
L "        row_act=np.sum(frame!=s._bg,axis=1)"
L "        for r in range(20,44):"
L "            if row_act[r]<=2 and np.sum(row_act[:r]>0)>=5 and np.sum(row_act[r+1:]>0)>=5:"
L "                for y in range(r+1):"
L "                    for x in range(64):mask[y*64+x]=0.05"
L "                return mask"
L "        return mask"
L ""
L "    def _reward(s, prev_raw, curr_raw, prev_h, curr_h):"
L "        mask=np.ones((64,64),dtype=bool);mask[:2]=False;mask[62:]=False"
L "        diff=(prev_raw!=curr_raw)&mask;changed=np.any(diff)"
L "        r=0.0"
L "        if curr_h!=prev_h:r+=1.5 if not hasattr(s,'_visited_hashes') else (1.5 if curr_h not in s._visited_hashes else 0.0)"
L "        elif curr_h==prev_h:r-=0.1"
L "        if changed:r+=0.5"
L "        curr_objs=fast_objects(curr_raw,s._bg)"
L "        if s._prev_objs and curr_objs:"
L "            moved=0"
L "            for co in curr_objs:"
L "                for po in s._prev_objs:"
L "                    if co[0]==po[0]:"
L "                        dist=abs(co[1]-po[1])+abs(co[2]-po[2])"
L "                        if 2<dist<20:moved+=1;break"
L "            if moved>0:r+=0.3*min(moved,3);s._obj_moved=moved"
L "        s._prev_objs=curr_objs"
L "        return r"
L ""
L "    def _sample(s, logits, avail=None, temp=1.0):"
L "        al=logits[:5].clone();cl=logits[5:5+4096].clone()"
L "        if avail is not None and len(avail)>0:"
L "            mask=torch.full_like(al,float('-inf'));a6=False"
L "            for a in avail:"
L "                aid=a.value if hasattr(a,'value') else int(a)"
L "                if 1<=aid<=5:mask[aid-1]=0.0"
L "                elif aid==6:a6=True"
L "            al=al+mask"
L "            if not a6:cl=cl+torch.full_like(cl,float('-inf'))"
L "        if s._wm is not None:cl=cl+torch.log(s._wm.to(s.device).clamp(min=0.01))"
L "        ap=torch.sigmoid(al/temp);cp=torch.sigmoid(cl/temp)/(s.G*s.G)"
L "        allp=torch.cat([ap,cp]);sm=allp.sum()"
L "        if sm<1e-8:allp=torch.ones_like(allp)/len(allp)"
L "        else:allp=allp/sm"
L "        idx=np.random.choice(len(allp),p=allp.cpu().numpy())"
L "        if idx<5:return idx,None"
L "        ci=idx-5;return 5,(ci//s.G,ci%s.G)"
L ""
L "    def _heuristic(s, frame, avail, step):"
L "        av=set(int(a.value) if hasattr(a,'value') else int(a) for a in avail)"
L "        for d in[1,2,3,4]:"
L "            if d in av and step<4:return d-1,None"
L "        if 6 in av:"
L "            cnt=np.bincount(frame.flatten(),minlength=16);targets=[]"
L "            for c in range(16):"
L "                if c==s._bg or cnt[c]==0 or cnt[c]>2000:continue"
L "                ys,xs=np.where(frame==c)"
L "                if len(ys)>=2:targets.append((int(np.median(xs)),int(np.median(ys)),len(ys)))"
L "            targets.sort(key=lambda t:t[2]);pidx=step-4"
L "            if 0<=pidx<len(targets):return 5,(targets[pidx][1],targets[pidx][0])"
L "        if 5 in av:return 4,None"
L "        choices=[a for a in av if 1<=a<=5]"
L "        if choices:return random.choice(choices)-1,None"
L "        return 0,None"
L ""
L "    def _frame_to_tensor(s, frame):"
L "        oh=torch.zeros(16,64,64,dtype=torch.float32)"
L "        oh.scatter_(0,torch.from_numpy(frame).unsqueeze(0),1)"
L "        cnt=np.bincount(frame.flatten(),minlength=16)"
L "        bg=int(cnt.argmax());mx=max(cnt.max(),1)"
L "        bg_m=(frame==bg).astype(np.float32)"
L "        rar=np.zeros((64,64),np.float32)"
L "        for c in range(16):"
L "            if cnt[c]>0:rar[frame==c]=1.0-cnt[c]/mx"
L "        pad=np.pad(frame,1,mode='edge')"
L "        edge=((frame!=pad[:-2,1:-1])|(frame!=pad[2:,1:-1])|(frame!=pad[1:-1,:-2])|(frame!=pad[1:-1,2:])).astype(np.float32)"
L "        rp=np.linspace(0,1,64,dtype=np.float32).reshape(64,1).repeat(64,1)"
L "        cp=np.linspace(0,1,64,dtype=np.float32).reshape(1,64).repeat(64,0)"
L "        aug=torch.from_numpy(np.stack([bg_m,rar,edge,rp,cp]))"
L "        zeros=torch.zeros(5,64,64,dtype=torch.float32)"
L "        return torch.cat([oh,aug,zeros],0)"
L ""
L "    def _train(s):"
L "        if len(s.buf)<s.bsz:return"
L "        indices=np.random.choice(len(s.buf),s.bsz,replace=False)"
L "        batch=[s.buf[i] for i in indices]"
L "        states=torch.stack([s._frame_to_tensor(e['s']).to(s.device) for e in batch])"
L "        acts=torch.tensor([e['a'] for e in batch],dtype=torch.long,device=s.device)"
L "        rews=torch.tensor([e['r'] for e in batch],dtype=torch.float32,device=s.device)"
L "        rews=torch.sigmoid(rews);s.opt.zero_grad()"
L "        logits=s.net(states)"
L "        acts_c=acts.clamp(0,logits.size(1)-1)"
L "        sel=logits.gather(1,acts_c.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)"
L "        loss=F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(sel,rews)"
L "        p=torch.sigmoid(logits);loss=loss-0.0001*p[:,:5].mean()-0.00001*p[:,5:].mean()"
L "        loss.backward();s.opt.step()"
L ""
L "    def _get_aem_tensors(s):"
L "        if len(s._aem_diffs)<2:return None,None,None"
L "        M=len(s._aem_diffs)"
L "        diffs=torch.zeros(1,M,1,64,64,device=s.device)"
L "        acts=torch.zeros(1,M,dtype=torch.long,device=s.device)"
L "        rews=torch.zeros(1,M,device=s.device)"
L "        for i,(d,a,r) in enumerate(zip(s._aem_diffs,s._aem_actions,s._aem_rewards)):"
L "            diffs[0,i,0]=torch.from_numpy(d.astype(np.float32));acts[0,i]=min(a,4);rews[0,i]=r"
L "        return diffs,acts,rews"
L ""
L "    def is_done(s, frames, lf):"
L "        try: return lf.state is GameState.WIN or (time.time()-s.start_time) >= 8*3600-300"
L "        except: return True"
L ""
L "    def choose_action(s, frames, lf):"
L "        try:"
L "            lvl = s._lvl(lf)"
L ""
L "            # ===== LEVEL CHANGE ====="
L "            if lvl != s.cl:"
L "                # Init BFS solver on first level"
L "                if not s._bfs_tried:"
L "                    s._bfs_tried = True"
L "                    s._init_bfs()"
L ""
L "                # Try BFS for this level"
L "                s._bfs_solution = None"
L "                s._bfs_step = 0"
L "                if s._bfs:"
L "                    s._try_bfs_solve(lvl)"
L ""
L "                # Init CNN fallback"
L "                s.buf.clear(); s.buf_h.clear()"
L "                s.net = ForgeNet(s.IN, s.G).to(s.device)"
L "                for wp in ['/kaggle/input/forge-pretrained-weights/pretrained_weights.pt',"
L "                           'pretrained_weights.pt']:"
L "                    try:"
L "                        if os.path.exists(wp):"
L "                            state=torch.load(wp,map_location=s.device,weights_only=True)"
L "                            ms=s.net.state_dict()"
L "                            for k in list(state.keys()):"
L "                                if k in ms and state[k].shape==ms[k].shape:ms[k]=state[k]"
L "                            s.net.load_state_dict(ms);break"
L "                    except: pass"
L "                s.opt = optim.Adam(s.net.parameters(), lr=0.0003)"
L "                s.pt=None;s.pai=None;s.pr=None;s.ph=None"
L "                s.cl=lvl;s.fhist.clear();s.la=0"
L "                s._wd=False;s._wm=None;s._eps=0.15"
L "                s._aem_diffs.clear();s._aem_actions.clear();s._aem_rewards.clear()"
L "                s._prev_objs=None;s._obj_moved=0;s._ckpt_hash=None;s._unproductive=0"
L ""
L "                # v18: CLTI — inject BFS L0 demos into CNN replay buffer"
L "                if lvl > 0 and s._bfs and s._bfs.solutions.get(lvl - 1):"
L "                    prev_sol = s._bfs.solutions[lvl - 1]"
L "                    try:"
L "                        replay_game = s._bfs.game_cls()"
L "                        replay_game.set_level(lvl - 1)"
L "                        replay_game.perform_action(ActionInput(id=GameAction.RESET), raw=True)"
L "                        replay_game.perform_action(ActionInput(id=GameAction.RESET), raw=True)"
L "                        for act_id, data in prev_sol:"
L "                            prev_frame = np.array(replay_game.get_pixels(0, 0, 64, 64), dtype=np.int64)"
L "                            ai = ActionInput(id=GameAction.from_id(act_id), data=data) if data else ActionInput(id=GameAction.from_id(act_id))"
L "                            replay_game.perform_action(ai, raw=True)"

L "class ForgeNet(nn.Module):"
L "    def __init__(s, in_ch=26, g=64):"

L "        s.c3 = nn.Conv2d(64, 128, 3, padding=1)"
L "        s.ap = nn.MaxPool2d(4, 4)"

L "        s.ah = nn.Linear(256, 5)"
L "        s.cc1 = nn.Conv2d(128, 64, 3, padding=1)"
L "        s.cc2 = nn.Conv2d(64, 1, 1)"

T GNP
L "        af = s.ap(f).reshape(f.size(0), -1)"
L "        al = s.ah(F.relu(s.af(af)))"
T GOP

L "def to_2d(obs_or_array):"
L "    if obs_or_array is None:"
L "        return np.zeros((64, 64), dtype=np.int32)"
L "    if hasattr(obs_or_array, 'frame'):"
L "        f = np.array(obs_or_array.frame, dtype=np.int32)"
L "    else:"
L "        f = np.asarray(obs_or_array, dtype=np.int32)"
L "    if f.ndim == 3:"
L "        f = f[-1]"
L "    if f.ndim != 2:"
L "        f = np.zeros((64, 64), dtype=np.int32)"
L "    return f"
L ""
L "def bg_color(frame):"
L "    return int(np.bincount(np.asarray(frame).flatten()).argmax())"
L ""
L "def bfs_path(grid, start, goal, walls):"
L "    if start == goal:"
L "        return []"
L "    h, w = grid.shape"
L "    q, vis = deque([(start, [])]), {start}"
L "    deltas = {1:(-1,0), 2:(1,0), 3:(0,-1), 4:(0,1)}"
L "    while q:"
L "        pos, path = q.popleft()"
L "        for act, (dr, dc) in deltas.items():"
L "            n = (pos[0] + dr, pos[1] + dc)"
L "            if 0 <= n[0] < h and 0 <= n[1] < w and n not in vis and not walls[n[0], n[1]]:"
L "                vis.add(n)"
L "                new_path = path + [act]"
L "                if n == goal:"
L "                    return new_path"
L "                q.append((n, new_path))"
L "    return []"
L ""
L "def lawnmower_scan(w=64, h=64, step=8):"
L "    pos = []"
L "    for i, y in enumerate(range(0, h, step)):"
L "        xs = range(0, w, step) if i % 2 == 0 else range(w - 1, -1, -step)"
L "        for x in xs:"
L "            pos.append((x, y))"
L "    return pos"
L ""
L "def gf2_solve(toggle_matrix, target_state):"
L "    n, m = len(target_state), len(toggle_matrix)"
L "    if m == 0 or n == 0:"
L "        return []"
L "    aug = np.zeros((n, m + 1), dtype=int)"
L "    for i in range(n):"
L "        for j in range(m):"
L "            aug[i, j] = int(toggle_matrix[j][i]) % 2"
L "        aug[i, m] = int(target_state[i]) % 2"
L "    pivot_cols, row = [], 0"
L "    for col in range(m):"
L "        found = next((r for r in range(row, n) if aug[r, col] == 1), -1)"
L "        if found == -1:"
L "            continue"
L "        aug[[row, found]] = aug[[found, row]]"
L "        for r in range(n):"
L "            if r != row and aug[r, col] == 1:"
L "                aug[r] = (aug[r] + aug[row]) % 2"
L "        pivot_cols.append(col)"
L "        row += 1"
L "    sol = np.zeros(m, dtype=int)"
L "    for i, col in enumerate(pivot_cols):"
L "        if i < n:"
L "            sol[col] = aug[i, m]"
L "    return [j for j in range(m) if sol[j] == 1]"
L ""
L "def detect_player_pos(prev_frame, cur_frame):"
L "    diff = (prev_frame != cur_frame)"
L "    if not diff.any():"
L "        return None"
L "    bg = bg_color(prev_frame)"
L "    rows, cols = np.where(diff)"
L "    for r, c in zip(rows, cols):"
L "        if prev_frame[r, c] == bg and cur_frame[r, c] != bg:"
L "            return (int(r), int(c))"
L "    return (int(rows.mean()), int(cols.mean()))"
L ""
L "class GameObserver:"
L "    def __init__(self):"
L "        self.jsonl = []"
L "        self.action_eff = defaultdict(list)"
L "        self.click_effs = []"
L "        self.toggle_map = {}"
L "        self.player_trail = []"
L "        self.player_color = None"
L "        self._bg = None"
L ""
L "    def start_phase(self, frame, avail):"
L "        self._bg = bg_color(frame)"
L ""
L "    def record(self, prev_frame, action, data, cur_frame, step, obs2=None):"
L "        changed = not np.array_equal(prev_frame, cur_frame)"
L "        diff_count = int(np.sum(prev_frame != cur_frame))"
L "        bg = self._bg if self._bg is not None else bg_color(prev_frame)"
L "        self.action_eff[action].append({'changed': changed, 'diff': diff_count})"
L ""
L "        if action in [1, 2, 3, 4] and changed:"
L "            diff_mask = (prev_frame != cur_frame)"
L "            appeared = {"
L "                int(cur_frame[r, c]) for r, c in zip(*np.where(diff_mask))"
L "                if prev_frame[r, c] == bg and cur_frame[r, c] != bg"
L "            }"
L "            disappeared = {"
L "                int(prev_frame[r, c]) for r, c in zip(*np.where(diff_mask))"
L "                if cur_frame[r, c] == bg and prev_frame[r, c] != bg"
L "            }"
L "            moving = appeared & disappeared or appeared"
L "            if moving:"
L "                mc = min(moving)"
L "                if self.player_color is None:"
L "                    self.player_color = mc"
L "                pos = detect_player_pos(prev_frame, cur_frame)"
L "                if pos:"
L "                    self.player_trail.append(pos)"
L ""
L "        if action == 6 and data:"
L "            x, y = int(data.get('x', 32)), int(data.get('y', 32))"
L "            r, c = min(y, prev_frame.shape[0] - 1), min(x, prev_frame.shape[1] - 1)"
L "            cb, ca = int(prev_frame[r, c]), int(cur_frame[r, c])"
L "            key = (x, y)"
L "            self.toggle_map.setdefault(key, []).append(ca)"
L "            is_toggle = len(set(self.toggle_map[key])) >= 2"
L "            self.click_effs.append({"
L "                'x': x, 'y': y, 'before': cb, 'after': ca,"
L "                'changed': changed, 'diff': diff_count, 'toggle': is_toggle, 'step': step,"
L "            })"
L ""
L "    def get_state(self, last_frame):"
L "        bg = bg_color(last_frame)"
L "        pc = self.player_color"
L "        player = self.player_trail[-1] if self.player_trail else None"
L ""
L "        color_counts = {}"
L "        for color in np.unique(last_frame):"
L "            if color == bg or (pc is not None and color == pc):"
L "                continue"
L "            cnt = int(np.sum(last_frame == color))"
L "            if 1 <= cnt <= 100:"
L "                color_counts[color] = cnt"
L ""
L "        targets = []"
L "        if color_counts:"
L "            goal_color = min(color_counts, key=color_counts.get)"
L "            targets = [tuple(p) for p in np.argwhere(last_frame == goal_color)]"
L "            for c, cnt in sorted(color_counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1]):"
L "                if c == goal_color:"
L "                    continue"
L "                if len(targets) >= 25:"
L "                    break"
L "                targets.extend([tuple(p) for p in np.argwhere(last_frame == c)])"
L ""
L "        walls = np.zeros((64, 64), dtype=bool)"
L "        return player, targets, walls"
L ""
L "class NavigateSpecialist:"
L "    def __init__(self, cls=None):"
L "        self._stuck = 0"
L "        self._prev_pos = None"
L "        self._dir_cycle = 0"
L ""
T GAM
L "        moves = [a for a in avail if a in [1, 2, 3, 4]]"
L "        if not moves:"
L "            return avail[0], None"
L "        if not player:"
L "            act = moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)]"
L "            self._dir_cycle += 1"
L "            return act, None"
L "        if not targets:"
L "            act = moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)]"
L "            self._dir_cycle += 1"
L "            return act, None"
L "        if self._prev_pos == player:"
L "            self._stuck += 1"
L "        else:"
L "            self._stuck = 0"
L "        self._prev_pos = player"
L "        if self._stuck > 8:"
L "            self._stuck = 0"
L "            self._dir_cycle += 1"
L "            return moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)], None"
L ""
L "        near = min(targets, key=lambda t: abs(t[0] - player[0]) + abs(t[1] - player[1]))"
L "        dist = abs(near[0] - player[0]) + abs(near[1] - player[1])"
L "        path = bfs_path(frame, player, tuple(near), walls)"
L "        if path:"
L "            return path[0], None"
L "        if dist <= 2 and 6 in avail:"
L "            return 6, {'x': int(near[1]), 'y': int(near[0])}"
L "        dr = near[0] - player[0]"
L "        dc = near[1] - player[1]"
L "        preferred = 2 if abs(dr) >= abs(dc) and dr > 0 else \\"
L "                    1 if abs(dr) >= abs(dc) else \\"
L "                    4 if dc > 0 else 3"
L "        if preferred in moves:"
L "            return preferred, None"
L "        return moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)], None"

L "def _frame_fp64(frame):"
L "    return hashlib.md5(np.asarray(frame).tobytes()).hexdigest()[:16]"

L "def _bbox_from_mask(mask):"
L "    ys, xs = np.where(mask)"
L "    if len(xs) == 0:"
L "        return None"
L "    return (int(xs.min()), int(ys.min()), int(xs.max()), int(ys.max()))"
L ""
L "def _mask_centroid(mask):"
L "    ys, xs = np.where(mask)"
L "    if len(xs) == 0:"
L "        return None"
L "    return (float(np.mean(xs)), float(np.mean(ys)))"
L ""
L "def _connected_components(mask):"
L "    h, w = mask.shape"
L "    vis = np.zeros_like(mask, dtype=bool)"
L "    comps = []"
L "    for y in range(h):"
L "        for x in range(w):"
L "            if not mask[y, x] or vis[y, x]:"
L "                continue"
L "            q = deque([(y, x)])"
L "            vis[y, x] = True"
L "            pts = []"
L "            while q:"
L "                cy, cx = q.popleft()"
L "                pts.append((cy, cx))"
L "                for ny, nx in ((cy-1, cx), (cy+1, cx), (cy, cx-1), (cy, cx+1)):"
L "                    if 0 <= ny < h and 0 <= nx < w and mask[ny, nx] and not vis[ny, nx]:"
L "                        vis[ny, nx] = True"
L "                        q.append((ny, nx))"
L "            comps.append(pts)"
L "    return comps"
L ""
L "def _component_mask(shape, pts):"
L "    m = np.zeros(shape, dtype=bool)"
L "    for y, x in pts:"
L "        m[y, x] = True"
L "    return m"
L ""
L "def _nearest_point(points, origin):"
L "    if not points:"
L "        return None"
L "    return min(points, key=lambda p: abs(p[0] - origin[0]) + abs(p[1] - origin[1]))"
L ""
L "def _neighbors4(p):"
L "    y, x = p"
L "    return [(y-1,x), (y+1,x), (y,x-1), (y,x+1)]"
L ""
L "def _find_reachable_goal_adjacent(grid, start, goal_cells, walls):"
L "    h, w = grid.shape"
L "    goal_adj = set()"
L "    for gy, gx in goal_cells:"
L "        for ny, nx in _neighbors4((gy, gx)):"
L "            if 0 <= ny < h and 0 <= nx < w and not walls[ny, nx]:"
L "                goal_adj.add((ny, nx))"
L "    if not goal_adj:"
L "        return []"
L "    q, vis = deque([(start, [])]), {start}"
L "    deltas = {1:(-1,0), 2:(1,0), 3:(0,-1), 4:(0,1)}"
L "    while q:"
L "        pos, path = q.popleft()"
L "        if pos in goal_adj:"
L "            return path"
L "        for act, (dy, dx) in deltas.items():"
L "            npy, npx = pos[0] + dy, pos[1] + dx"
L "            nxt = (npy, npx)"
L "            if 0 <= npy < h and 0 <= npx < w and nxt not in vis and not walls[npy, npx]:"
L "                vis.add(nxt)"
L "                q.append((nxt, path + [act]))"
L "    return []"
L ""
L "def _path_to_exact(grid, start, target, walls):"
L "    return bfs_path(grid, start, target, walls)"
L ""
L "def _ls20_budget_estimate(frame):"
L "    # Large yellow bar near bottom. Return rough bucketized budget estimate."
L "    arr = np.asarray(frame)"
L "    h, w = arr.shape"
L "    y0 = max(0, h - 10)"
L "    band = arr[y0:h, :]"
L "    vals, counts = np.unique(band, return_counts=True)"
L "    if len(vals) == 0:"
L "        return {\"bucket\": \"unknown\", \"fill_ratio\": 0.0, \"color\": None}"
L "    yellowish = int(vals[np.argmax(counts)])"
L "    row_counts = np.sum(band == yellowish, axis=1)"
L "    best_row = int(np.argmax(row_counts))"
L "    row = band[best_row]"
L "    filled = int(np.sum(row == yellowish))"
L "    fill_ratio = filled / max(1, len(row))"
L "    if fill_ratio < 0.18:"
L "        bucket = \"low\""
L "    elif fill_ratio < 0.45:"
L "        bucket = \"mid\""
L "    else:"
L "        bucket = \"high\""
L "    return {\"bucket\": bucket, \"fill_ratio\": float(fill_ratio), \"color\": yellowish}"
L ""
L "def _ls20_walkable_and_walls(frame):"
L "    # Use border-dominant bg as walkable floor prior, darkest dense regions as walls."
L "    arr = np.asarray(frame)"
L "    bg = _get_bg(arr)"
L "    walls = np.zeros_like(arr, dtype=bool)"
L ""
L "    vals, counts = np.unique(arr, return_counts=True)"
L "    common = sorted(zip(counts.tolist(), vals.tolist()), reverse=True)"
L "    candidate_wall_colors = [v for _, v in common[:4] if v != bg]"
L ""
L "    for c in candidate_wall_colors:"
L "        mask = (arr == c)"
L "        comps = _connected_components(mask)"
L "        for pts in comps:"
L "            if len(pts) >= 20:"
L "                cm = _component_mask(arr.shape, pts)"
L "                bb = _bbox_from_mask(cm)"
L "                if bb is None:"
L "                    continue"
L "                x0, y0, x1, y1 = bb"
L "                ww, hh = x1 - x0 + 1, y1 - y0 + 1"
L "                # Large solid-ish rectangles are likely walls/door housing."
L "                if ww >= 2 and hh >= 2:"
L "                    walls |= cm"
L ""
L "    # Do not wall off the outer floor completely."
L "    walls[0, :] = False"
L "    walls[-1, :] = False"
L "    walls[:, 0] = False"
L "    walls[:, -1] = False"
L "    return bg, walls"
L ""
L "def _ls20_decode_objects(frame, prev_player=None):"
L "    arr = np.asarray(frame)"
L "    h, w = arr.shape"
L "    bg, walls = _ls20_walkable_and_walls(arr)"
L ""
L "    # Find player via orange/cyan two-color mover if possible."
L "    ents = extract_entities(arr, bg)"
L "    player = None"
L "    best_player_score = None"
L "    for e in ents:"
L "        colors = set(e[\"colors\"])"
L "        score = 0"
L "        if len(colors) == 2:"
L "            score += 3"
L "        if len(colors) in (1, 2, 3):"
L "            score += 1"
L "        if 2 <= sum(e[\"color_counts\"].values()) <= 20:"
L "            score += 2"
L "        py, px = e[\"y\"], e[\"x\"]"
L "        if 0 < py < h-1 and 0 < px < w-1:"
L "            score += 1"
L "        if prev_player is not None:"
L "            score -= 0.1 * (abs(py - prev_player[0]) + abs(px - prev_player[1]))"
L "        if best_player_score is None or score > best_player_score:"
L "            best_player_score = score"
L "            player = (int(round(py)), int(round(px)))"
L ""
L "    # Cross: small white-ish plus near open area."
L "    cross_positions = []"
L "    vals, counts = np.unique(arr, return_counts=True)"
L "    for c, cnt in zip(vals.tolist(), counts.tolist()):"
L "        if 1 <= cnt <= 20:"
L "            mask = (arr == c)"
L "            comps = _connected_components(mask)"
L "            for pts in comps:"
L "                if 4 <= len(pts) <= 9:"
L "                    cm = _component_mask(arr.shape, pts)"
L "                    bb = _bbox_from_mask(cm)"
L "                    if bb is None:"
L "                        continue"
L "                    x0, y0, x1, y1 = bb"
L "                    ww, hh = x1 - x0 + 1, y1 - y0 + 1"
L "                    if ww <= 5 and hh <= 5:"
L "                        # plus-like if center has 4-neighbor support"
L "                        ys, xs = np.where(cm)"
L "                        cy, cx = int(round(np.mean(ys))), int(round(np.mean(xs)))"
L "                        n4 = 0"
L "                        for ny, nx in _neighbors4((cy, cx)):"
L "                            if 0 <= ny < h and 0 <= nx < w and cm[ny, nx]:"
L "                                n4 += 1"
L "                        if n4 >= 3:"
L "                            cross_positions.append((cy, cx))"
L ""
L "    # Refill: medium yellow square-ish blobs."
L "    refill_positions = []"
L "    common_colors = [v for _, v in common] if (common := sorted(zip(counts.tolist(), vals.tolist()), reverse=True)) else []"
L "    for c in common_colors[:8]:"
L "        if c == bg:"
L "            continue"
L "        mask = (arr == c)"
L "        comps = _connected_components(mask)"
L "        for pts in comps:"
L "            if 6 <= len(pts) <= 80:"
L "                cm = _component_mask(arr.shape, pts)"
L "                bb = _bbox_from_mask(cm)"
L "                if bb is None:"
L "                    continue"
L "                x0, y0, x1, y1 = bb"
L "                ww, hh = x1 - x0 + 1, y1 - y0 + 1"
L "                area = ww * hh"
L "                if 2 <= ww <= 8 and 2 <= hh <= 8 and len(pts) >= max(4, area // 2):"
L "                    cy, cx = _mask_centroid(cm)"
L "                    refill_positions.append((int(round(cy)), int(round(cx))))"
L ""
L "    # Door region: dense dark component with a smaller icon-colored component embedded."
L "    door_positions = []"
L "    for c in common_colors[:6]:"
L "        if c == bg:"
L "            continue"
L "        mask = (arr == c)"
L "        comps = _connected_components(mask)"
L "        for pts in comps:"
L "            if len(pts) >= 20:"
L "                cm = _component_mask(arr.shape, pts)"
L "                bb = _bbox_from_mask(cm)"
L "                if bb is None:"
L "                    continue"
L "                x0, y0, x1, y1 = bb"
L "                ww, hh = x1 - x0 + 1, y1 - y0 + 1"
L "                if 4 <= ww <= 16 and 4 <= hh <= 16:"
L "                    door_positions.append((int(round((y0+y1)/2)), int(round((x0+x1)/2))))"
L ""
L "    # Deduplicate near-identical object centers."
L "    def _dedup(points, radius=2):"
L "        out = []"
L "        for p in points:"
L "            if all(abs(p[0]-q[0]) + abs(p[1]-q[1]) > radius for q in out):"
L "                out.append(p)"
L "        return out"
L ""
L "    cross_positions = _dedup(cross_positions)"
L "    refill_positions = _dedup(refill_positions)"
L "    door_positions = _dedup(door_positions)"
L ""
L "    return {"
L "        \"bg\": bg,"
L "        \"walls\": walls,"
L "        \"player\": player,"
L "        \"crosses\": cross_positions,"
L "        \"refills\": refill_positions,"
L "        \"doors\": door_positions,"
L "        \"budget\": _ls20_budget_estimate(arr),"
L "    }"
L ""
L "def _ls20_choose_phase_action(agent, raw, avail_ids, step):"
L "    st = agent._ls20"
L "    fp = _frame_fp64(raw)"
L ""
L "    if fp == _LS20_LEVEL1_FP:"
L "        if st[\"replay_fp\"] != fp:"
L "            st[\"replay_fp\"] = fp"
L "            st[\"replay_seq\"] = list(_LS20_LEVEL1_SOL)"
L "            st[\"replay_idx\"] = 0"
L "        if st[\"replay_idx\"] < len(st[\"replay_seq\"]):"
L "            act = st[\"replay_seq\"][st[\"replay_idx\"]]"
L "            st[\"replay_idx\"] += 1"
L "            return act, None"
L ""
L "    objs = _ls20_decode_objects(raw, prev_player=st.get(\"last_player\"))"
L "    player = objs[\"player\"]"
L "    crosses = objs[\"crosses\"]"
L "    refills = objs[\"refills\"]"
L "    doors = objs[\"doors\"]"
L "    walls = objs[\"walls\"]"
L "    budget = objs[\"budget\"]"
L "    st[\"last_player\"] = player"
L "    st[\"last_fp\"] = fp"
L ""
L "    # Persist discovered objects across frames/levels."
L "    if crosses:"
L "        st[\"cross_positions\"] = crosses"
L "    else:"
L "        crosses = st.get(\"cross_positions\", [])"
L "    if refills:"
L "        st[\"refill_positions\"] = refills"
L "    else:"
L "        refills = st.get(\"refill_positions\", [])"
L "    if doors:"
L "        st[\"door_positions\"] = doors"
L "    else:"
L "        doors = st.get(\"door_positions\", [])"
L ""
L "    moves = [a for a in avail_ids if a in [1, 2, 3, 4]]"
L "    if not moves:"
L "        return (avail_ids[0] if avail_ids else 1), None"
L ""
L "    if player is None:"
L "        # low-entropy scan when player not yet reliably detected"
L "        return moves[step % len(moves)], None"
L ""
L "    # Update simple icon-phase guess using cross contact / visits."
L "    if crosses:"
L "        near_cross = min(abs(player[0]-c[0]) + abs(player[1]-c[1]) for c in crosses)"
L "        if near_cross <= 1 and st.get(\"last_cross_step\") != step:"
L "            st[\"icon_phase_guess\"] = (st[\"icon_phase_guess\"] + 1) % max(1, st[\"phase_count_guess\"])"
L "            st[\"last_cross_step\"] = step"
L ""
L "    # If we are low on budget, route to refill first when reachable."
L "    if refills and budget[\"bucket\"] == \"low\":"
L "        tgt = _nearest_point(refills, player)"
L "        if tgt is not None:"
L "            path = _path_to_exact(raw, player, tgt, walls)"
L "            if path:"
L "                return path[0], None"
L ""
L "    # Door testing policy: after each phase bump, try door immediately."
L "    if doors:"
L "        dpath = _find_reachable_goal_adjacent(raw, player, doors, walls)"
L "        if dpath:"
L "            # If short or budget OK, commit."
L "            if len(dpath) <= 8 or budget[\"bucket\"] != \"low\":"
L "                return dpath[0], None"
L ""
L "    # If cross exists, go there to advance icon phase."
L "    if crosses:"
L "        ctarget = _nearest_point(crosses, player)"
L "        if ctarget is not None:"
L "            cpath = _path_to_exact(raw, player, ctarget, walls)"
L "            if cpath:"
L "                return cpath[0], None"
L ""
L "    # If budget not low and refill exists, use it opportunistically only when nearby."
L "    if refills:"
L "        rtgt = _nearest_point(refills, player)"
L "        if rtgt is not None:"
L "            md = abs(rtgt[0]-player[0]) + abs(rtgt[1]-player[1])"
L "            if md <= 6:"
L "                rpath = _path_to_exact(raw, player, rtgt, walls)"
L "                if rpath:"
L "                    return rpath[0], None"
L ""
L "    # Finally, if door known, keep pushing toward it."
L "    if doors:"
L "        dpath = _find_reachable_goal_adjacent(raw, player, doors, walls)"
L "        if dpath:"
L "            return dpath[0], None"
L ""
L "    # Deterministic fallback."
L "    avoid = _REVERSAL_PAIRS.get(agent._prev_action_id)"
L "    pool = [a for a in moves if a != avoid] or moves"
L "    return pool[step % len(pool)], None"
L ""
