L "    def _pick_action(s, raw, avail_ids, step):"
L "        \"\"\""
L "        Decision priority:"
L "          1. WM entity plan (player+goal+movers known)"
L "          2. FDM exploitation (strong signal: 80% best action)"

L "def _looks_like_env_root(d: Path) -> bool:"

T GAD
L "def _kaggle_dataset_mounts(inp: Path) -> list[Path]:"
L "    '''All dataset mount dirs under /kaggle/input."
L ""
L "    Kaggle uses several nesting conventions:"
L "      <slug>/                          — standard notebook input"
L "      datasets/<user>/<slug>/          — API-added datasets"
L "      competitions/<competition>/      — competition data"
L "    '''"
L "    mounts: list[Path] = []"
L "    for d in inp.iterdir():"
L "        if not d.is_dir():"
L "            continue"
L "        if d.name == \"datasets\":"
L "            for user_d in d.iterdir():"
L "                if user_d.is_dir():"
L "                    mounts.extend(sd for sd in user_d.iterdir() if sd.is_dir())"
L "        elif d.name == \"competitions\":"
L "            mounts.extend(sd for sd in d.iterdir() if sd.is_dir())"
L "        else:"
L "            mounts.append(d)"
L "    return sorted(mounts)"
L ""
T GAD
L "def _check_mount(ds: Path) -> Path | None:"
L "    '''Return the environment_files path from a dataset mount, or None.'''"
L "    if not ds.is_dir():"
L "        return None"
L "    ef = ds / \"environment_files\""
L "    if ef.is_dir():"
L "        return ef"
L "    if _looks_like_env_root(ds):"
L "        return ds"
L "    return None"
L ""
T GAD
L "def _find_all_env_dirs(inp: Path) -> list[Path]:"
L "    '''Return every environment_files directory found under /kaggle/input.'''"
L "    found: list[Path] = []"
L "    for slug in (\"arc-interactive-community\", \"arc-interactive\"):"
L "        for ds in [inp / slug] + list(inp.glob(f\"datasets/*/{slug}\")):"
L "            hit = _check_mount(ds)"
L "            if hit is not None and hit not in found:"
L "                found.append(hit)"
L "    for d in _kaggle_dataset_mounts(inp):"
L "        hit = _check_mount(d)"
L "        if hit is not None and hit not in found:"
L "            found.append(hit)"
L "    return found"
L ""

L "def _game_id_for_stem(stem: str) -> tuple[str, str]:"
L "    '''Return (game_id, env_dir) for a stem across all ENV_DIRS.'''"
L "    for d in ENV_DIRS:"
L "        base = Path(d) / stem"
L "        if not base.is_dir():"
L "            continue"
L "        for vd in sorted(base.iterdir(), key=lambda p: p.name.lower()):"
L "            if not vd.is_dir():"
L "                continue"
L "            mp = vd / \"metadata.json\""
L "            if mp.is_file():"
L "                data = json.loads(mp.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))"
L "                gid = data.get(\"game_id\")"
L "                if isinstance(gid, str):"
L "                    return gid, str(d)"
L "    raise FileNotFoundError(f\"stem {stem!r} not found in {[str(d) for d in ENV_DIRS]}\")"

L "gid, env_dir = _game_id_for_stem(STEM)"
L "arc = Arcade(environments_dir=env_dir, operation_mode=OperationMode.OFFLINE)"
L "env = arc.make(gid, seed=0, render_mode=None)"
L "if env is None:"
L "    raise RuntimeError(\"arc.make returned None\")"
L "obs = env.reset()"
L "n = 0"
L "for t in range(MAX_STEPS):"
L "    if _terminal(obs):"
L "        break"
L "    a = random.choice(POOL)"
L "    obs = env.step(a, reasoning={\"step\": t + 1})"
L "    n += 1"
T GMU

L "            elif current_val == goal_val:"
L "                return []"
L ""
T GGS
T GIM
L "            best_aid = None"
L "            best_closeness = float('inf')"
L ""
T GAU
L "                d = deltas.get(self.goal_field, None)"
L "                if d is None: continue"
L ""
L "                if isinstance(d, float):"
L "                    # Numeric delta"
L "                    new_val = current_val + d"
T GAV
L "                        if new_val in goal_val:"
L "                            # Hits target in 1 step — ideal"
T GAS
T GMM
T GHW
L "                    else:"
L "                        closeness = abs(new_val - goal_val)"
L "                    if closeness < best_closeness:"
T GQQ
L "                else:"
T GRT
T GAV
L "                        if d in goal_val:"
T GAS
L ""
L "            if best_aid is None:"
L "                return []"
L ""
T GLM
T GIN
L "            if isinstance(d, float) and d != 0:"
L "                if isinstance(goal_val, (list, tuple, set)):"
L "                    # Aim for closest target value"
T GGE
L "                else:"
L "                    target = goal_val"
T GGT
L "            else:"
L "                n_steps = 10  # non-numeric: just try 10 times"
L ""
L "            n_steps = min(n_steps, 300)"
L "            plan = [(best_aid, None)] * n_steps"
L "            self._log.info("
T GPS
L "                f\"(current={current_val} target={goal_val})\")"
L "            return plan"
L ""
T GAC
T GNO
L "        return []"
L ""
L "    def _read_nested(self, obj, path):"
T GHF
L "        try:"
L "            import re"
L "            parts = re.split(r'\\.', path)"
L "            current = obj"
L "            gsbn = getattr(obj, 'get_sprites_by_name', None)"
L "            for part in parts:"
L "                m = re.match(r'^(\\w+)\\[(\\d+)\\]$', part)"
L "                if m:"
L "                    name, idx = m.group(1), int(m.group(2))"
T GEQ
L "                    if callable(gsbn):"
L "                        try:"
L "                            sprites = gsbn(name)"
L "                            if sprites and idx < len(sprites):"
L "                                current = sprites[idx]"
L "                                continue"
T GAX
L "                            pass"
L "                    current = getattr(current, name)[idx]"
L "                else:"
L "                    current = getattr(current, part)"
T GEF
T GEK
L "            return None"
L ""

T GAA
L "# CNN FALLBACK (ForgeNet)"
T GRU
L "# Trained online using rewards shaped by WorldModel priors."
T GAA
L "class ForgeNet(nn.Module):"
L "    def __init__(s, in_ch=26, g=64):"
L "        super().__init__()"
L "        s.c1 = nn.Conv2d(in_ch, 32, 3, padding=1)"
L "        s.c2 = nn.Conv2d(32, 64, 3, padding=1)"
L "        s.c3 = nn.Conv2d(64, 128, 3, padding=1)"
L "        s.ap = nn.MaxPool2d(4, 4)"
L "        s.af = nn.Linear(128 * 16 * 16, 256)"
L "        s.ah = nn.Linear(256, 5)"
L "        s.cc1 = nn.Conv2d(128, 64, 3, padding=1)"
L "        s.cc2 = nn.Conv2d(64, 1, 1)"
L ""
L "    def forward(s, x):"
T GNP
L "        af = s.ap(f).reshape(f.size(0), -1)"
L "        al = s.ah(F.relu(s.af(af)))"
T GOP
L "        return torch.cat([al, cl], 1)"
L ""
L ""
T GAA
L "# MAIN AGENT"
T GAA
L ""
T GAA
L "# SELECTIVE VISUAL FALLBACK HELPERS"
L "# Grafted from llm-visual-analyzer-arc-agi-3.ipynb, stripped of LLM/runtime glue"
T GAA
L ""
L "def to_2d(obs_or_array):"
L "    if obs_or_array is None:"
L "        return np.zeros((64, 64), dtype=np.int32)"
L "    if hasattr(obs_or_array, 'frame'):"
L "        f = np.array(obs_or_array.frame, dtype=np.int32)"
L "    else:"
L "        f = np.asarray(obs_or_array, dtype=np.int32)"
L "    if f.ndim == 3:"
L "        f = f[-1]"
L "    if f.ndim != 2:"
L "        f = np.zeros((64, 64), dtype=np.int32)"
L "    return f"
L ""
L "def bg_color(frame):"
L "    return int(np.bincount(np.asarray(frame).flatten()).argmax())"
L ""
L "def bfs_path(grid, start, goal, walls):"
L "    if start == goal:"
L "        return []"
L "    h, w = grid.shape"
L "    q, vis = deque([(start, [])]), {start}"
L "    deltas = {1:(-1,0), 2:(1,0), 3:(0,-1), 4:(0,1)}"
L "    while q:"
L "        pos, path = q.popleft()"
L "        for act, (dr, dc) in deltas.items():"
L "            n = (pos[0] + dr, pos[1] + dc)"
L "            if 0 <= n[0] < h and 0 <= n[1] < w and n not in vis and not walls[n[0], n[1]]:"
L "                vis.add(n)"
L "                new_path = path + [act]"
L "                if n == goal:"
L "                    return new_path"
L "                q.append((n, new_path))"
L "    return []"
L ""
L "def lawnmower_scan(w=64, h=64, step=8):"
L "    pos = []"
L "    for i, y in enumerate(range(0, h, step)):"
L "        xs = range(0, w, step) if i % 2 == 0 else range(w - 1, -1, -step)"
L "        for x in xs:"
L "            pos.append((x, y))"
L "    return pos"
L ""
L "def gf2_solve(toggle_matrix, target_state):"
L "    n, m = len(target_state), len(toggle_matrix)"
L "    if m == 0 or n == 0:"
L "        return []"
L "    aug = np.zeros((n, m + 1), dtype=int)"
L "    for i in range(n):"
L "        for j in range(m):"
L "            aug[i, j] = int(toggle_matrix[j][i]) % 2"
L "        aug[i, m] = int(target_state[i]) % 2"
L "    pivot_cols, row = [], 0"
L "    for col in range(m):"
L "        found = next((r for r in range(row, n) if aug[r, col] == 1), -1)"
L "        if found == -1:"
L "            continue"
L "        aug[[row, found]] = aug[[found, row]]"
L "        for r in range(n):"
L "            if r != row and aug[r, col] == 1:"
L "                aug[r] = (aug[r] + aug[row]) % 2"
L "        pivot_cols.append(col)"
L "        row += 1"
L "    sol = np.zeros(m, dtype=int)"
L "    for i, col in enumerate(pivot_cols):"
L "        if i < n:"
L "            sol[col] = aug[i, m]"
L "    return [j for j in range(m) if sol[j] == 1]"
L ""
L "def detect_player_pos(prev_frame, cur_frame):"
L "    diff = (prev_frame != cur_frame)"
L "    if not diff.any():"
L "        return None"
L "    bg = bg_color(prev_frame)"
L "    rows, cols = np.where(diff)"
L "    for r, c in zip(rows, cols):"
L "        if prev_frame[r, c] == bg and cur_frame[r, c] != bg:"
L "            return (int(r), int(c))"
L "    return (int(rows.mean()), int(cols.mean()))"
L ""
L "class GameObserver:"
L "    def __init__(self):"
L "        self.jsonl = []"
L "        self.action_eff = defaultdict(list)"
L "        self.click_effs = []"
L "        self.toggle_map = {}"
L "        self.player_trail = []"
L "        self.player_color = None"
L "        self._bg = None"
L ""
L "    def start_phase(self, frame, avail):"
L "        self._bg = bg_color(frame)"
L ""
L "    def record(self, prev_frame, action, data, cur_frame, step, obs2=None):"
L "        changed = not np.array_equal(prev_frame, cur_frame)"
L "        diff_count = int(np.sum(prev_frame != cur_frame))"
L "        bg = self._bg if self._bg is not None else bg_color(prev_frame)"
L "        self.action_eff[action].append({'changed': changed, 'diff': diff_count})"
L ""
L "        if action in [1, 2, 3, 4] and changed:"
L "            diff_mask = (prev_frame != cur_frame)"
L "            appeared = {"
L "                int(cur_frame[r, c]) for r, c in zip(*np.where(diff_mask))"
L "                if prev_frame[r, c] == bg and cur_frame[r, c] != bg"
L "            }"
L "            disappeared = {"
L "                int(prev_frame[r, c]) for r, c in zip(*np.where(diff_mask))"
L "                if cur_frame[r, c] == bg and prev_frame[r, c] != bg"
L "            }"
L "            moving = appeared & disappeared or appeared"
L "            if moving:"
L "                mc = min(moving)"
L "                if self.player_color is None:"
L "                    self.player_color = mc"
L "                pos = detect_player_pos(prev_frame, cur_frame)"
L "                if pos:"
L "                    self.player_trail.append(pos)"
L ""
L "        if action == 6 and data:"
L "            x, y = int(data.get('x', 32)), int(data.get('y', 32))"
L "            r, c = min(y, prev_frame.shape[0] - 1), min(x, prev_frame.shape[1] - 1)"
L "            cb, ca = int(prev_frame[r, c]), int(cur_frame[r, c])"
L "            key = (x, y)"
L "            self.toggle_map.setdefault(key, []).append(ca)"
L "            is_toggle = len(set(self.toggle_map[key])) >= 2"
L "            self.click_effs.append({"
L "                'x': x, 'y': y, 'before': cb, 'after': ca,"
L "                'changed': changed, 'diff': diff_count, 'toggle': is_toggle, 'step': step,"
L "            })"
L ""
L "    def get_state(self, last_frame):"
L "        bg = bg_color(last_frame)"
L "        pc = self.player_color"
L "        player = self.player_trail[-1] if self.player_trail else None"
L ""
L "        color_counts = {}"
L "        for color in np.unique(last_frame):"
L "            if color == bg or (pc is not None and color == pc):"
L "                continue"
L "            cnt = int(np.sum(last_frame == color))"
L "            if 1 <= cnt <= 100:"
L "                color_counts[color] = cnt"
L ""
L "        targets = []"
L "        if color_counts:"
L "            goal_color = min(color_counts, key=color_counts.get)"
L "            targets = [tuple(p) for p in np.argwhere(last_frame == goal_color)]"
L "            for c, cnt in sorted(color_counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1]):"
L "                if c == goal_color:"
L "                    continue"
L "                if len(targets) >= 25:"
L "                    break"
L "                targets.extend([tuple(p) for p in np.argwhere(last_frame == c)])"
L ""
L "        walls = np.zeros((64, 64), dtype=bool)"
L "        return player, targets, walls"
L ""
L "class NavigateSpecialist:"
L "    def __init__(self, cls=None):"
L "        self._stuck = 0"
L "        self._prev_pos = None"
L "        self._dir_cycle = 0"
L ""
T GAM
L "        moves = [a for a in avail if a in [1, 2, 3, 4]]"
L "        if not moves:"
L "            return avail[0], None"
L "        if not player:"
L "            act = moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)]"
L "            self._dir_cycle += 1"
L "            return act, None"
L "        if not targets:"
L "            act = moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)]"
L "            self._dir_cycle += 1"
L "            return act, None"
L "        if self._prev_pos == player:"
L "            self._stuck += 1"
L "        else:"
L "            self._stuck = 0"
L "        self._prev_pos = player"
L "        if self._stuck > 8:"
L "            self._stuck = 0"
L "            self._dir_cycle += 1"
L "            return moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)], None"
L ""
L "        near = min(targets, key=lambda t: abs(t[0] - player[0]) + abs(t[1] - player[1]))"
L "        dist = abs(near[0] - player[0]) + abs(near[1] - player[1])"
L "        path = bfs_path(frame, player, tuple(near), walls)"
L "        if path:"
L "            return path[0], None"
L "        if dist <= 2 and 6 in avail:"
L "            return 6, {'x': int(near[1]), 'y': int(near[0])}"
L "        dr = near[0] - player[0]"
L "        dc = near[1] - player[1]"
L "        preferred = 2 if abs(dr) >= abs(dc) and dr > 0 else \\"
L "                    1 if abs(dr) >= abs(dc) else \\"
L "                    4 if dc > 0 else 3"
L "        if preferred in moves:"
L "            return preferred, None"
L "        return moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)], None"
L ""
L "class LawnmowerSpecialist:"
L "    def __init__(self, cls=None, step=8):"

L "        self._clicked = set()"

T GQZ
T GEJ

L "            x, y = unclicked[0]"
L "            self._clicked.add((x, y))"
L "            return 6, {'x': x, 'y': y}"
L "        while self._idx < len(self.positions):"
L "            x, y = self.positions[self._idx]"
L "            self._idx += 1"
L "            if (x, y) not in self._clicked:"
L "                self._clicked.add((x, y))"
L "                return 6, {'x': x, 'y': y}"

L "        x, y = self.positions[turn % len(self.positions)]"
L "        return 6, {'x': x, 'y': y}"

L "def _looks_like_env_root(d: Path) -> bool:"

T GAD
L "def _kaggle_dataset_mounts(inp: Path) -> list[Path]:"
L "    '''All dataset mount dirs under /kaggle/input."
L ""
L "    Kaggle uses several nesting conventions:"
L "      <slug>/                          — standard notebook input"
L "      datasets/<user>/<slug>/          — API-added datasets"
L "      competitions/<competition>/      — competition data"
L "    '''"
L "    mounts: list[Path] = []"
L "    for d in inp.iterdir():"
L "        if not d.is_dir():"
L "            continue"
L "        if d.name == \"datasets\":"
L "            for user_d in d.iterdir():"
L "                if user_d.is_dir():"
L "                    mounts.extend(sd for sd in user_d.iterdir() if sd.is_dir())"
L "        elif d.name == \"competitions\":"
L "            mounts.extend(sd for sd in d.iterdir() if sd.is_dir())"
L "        else:"
L "            mounts.append(d)"
L "    return sorted(mounts)"
L ""
T GAD

L "    '''Return the environment_files path from a dataset mount, or None.'''"

L "def to_2d(obs_or_array):"
L "    if obs_or_array is None:"
L "        return np.zeros((64, 64), dtype=np.int32)"
L "    if hasattr(obs_or_array, 'frame'):"
L "        f = np.array(obs_or_array.frame, dtype=np.int32)"
L "    else:"
L "        f = np.asarray(obs_or_array, dtype=np.int32)"
L "    if f.ndim == 3:"
L "        f = f[-1]"
L "    if f.ndim != 2:"
L "        f = np.zeros((64, 64), dtype=np.int32)"
L "    return f"
L ""
L "def bg_color(frame):"
L "    return int(np.bincount(np.asarray(frame).flatten()).argmax())"
L ""
L "def bfs_path(grid, start, goal, walls):"
L "    if start == goal:"
L "        return []"
L "    h, w = grid.shape"
L "    q, vis = deque([(start, [])]), {start}"
L "    deltas = {1:(-1,0), 2:(1,0), 3:(0,-1), 4:(0,1)}"
L "    while q:"
L "        pos, path = q.popleft()"
L "        for act, (dr, dc) in deltas.items():"
L "            n = (pos[0] + dr, pos[1] + dc)"
L "            if 0 <= n[0] < h and 0 <= n[1] < w and n not in vis and not walls[n[0], n[1]]:"
L "                vis.add(n)"
L "                new_path = path + [act]"
L "                if n == goal:"
L "                    return new_path"
L "                q.append((n, new_path))"
L "    return []"
L ""
L "def lawnmower_scan(w=64, h=64, step=8):"
L "    pos = []"
L "    for i, y in enumerate(range(0, h, step)):"
L "        xs = range(0, w, step) if i % 2 == 0 else range(w - 1, -1, -step)"
L "        for x in xs:"
L "            pos.append((x, y))"
L "    return pos"
L ""
L "def gf2_solve(toggle_matrix, target_state):"
L "    n, m = len(target_state), len(toggle_matrix)"
L "    if m == 0 or n == 0:"
L "        return []"
L "    aug = np.zeros((n, m + 1), dtype=int)"
L "    for i in range(n):"
L "        for j in range(m):"
L "            aug[i, j] = int(toggle_matrix[j][i]) % 2"
L "        aug[i, m] = int(target_state[i]) % 2"
L "    pivot_cols, row = [], 0"
L "    for col in range(m):"
L "        found = next((r for r in range(row, n) if aug[r, col] == 1), -1)"
L "        if found == -1:"
L "            continue"
L "        aug[[row, found]] = aug[[found, row]]"
L "        for r in range(n):"
L "            if r != row and aug[r, col] == 1:"
L "                aug[r] = (aug[r] + aug[row]) % 2"
L "        pivot_cols.append(col)"
L "        row += 1"
L "    sol = np.zeros(m, dtype=int)"
L "    for i, col in enumerate(pivot_cols):"
L "        if i < n:"
L "            sol[col] = aug[i, m]"
L "    return [j for j in range(m) if sol[j] == 1]"
L ""
L "def detect_player_pos(prev_frame, cur_frame):"
L "    diff = (prev_frame != cur_frame)"
L "    if not diff.any():"
L "        return None"
L "    bg = bg_color(prev_frame)"
L "    rows, cols = np.where(diff)"
L "    for r, c in zip(rows, cols):"
L "        if prev_frame[r, c] == bg and cur_frame[r, c] != bg:"
L "            return (int(r), int(c))"
L "    return (int(rows.mean()), int(cols.mean()))"
L ""
L "class GameObserver:"
L "    def __init__(self):"
L "        self.jsonl = []"
L "        self.action_eff = defaultdict(list)"
L "        self.click_effs = []"
L "        self.toggle_map = {}"
L "        self.player_trail = []"
L "        self.player_color = None"
L "        self._bg = None"
L ""
L "    def start_phase(self, frame, avail):"
L "        self._bg = bg_color(frame)"
L ""
L "    def record(self, prev_frame, action, data, cur_frame, step, obs2=None):"
L "        changed = not np.array_equal(prev_frame, cur_frame)"
L "        diff_count = int(np.sum(prev_frame != cur_frame))"
L "        bg = self._bg if self._bg is not None else bg_color(prev_frame)"
L "        self.action_eff[action].append({'changed': changed, 'diff': diff_count})"
L ""
L "        if action in [1, 2, 3, 4] and changed:"
L "            diff_mask = (prev_frame != cur_frame)"
L "            appeared = {"
L "                int(cur_frame[r, c]) for r, c in zip(*np.where(diff_mask))"
L "                if prev_frame[r, c] == bg and cur_frame[r, c] != bg"
L "            }"
L "            disappeared = {"
L "                int(prev_frame[r, c]) for r, c in zip(*np.where(diff_mask))"
L "                if cur_frame[r, c] == bg and prev_frame[r, c] != bg"
L "            }"
L "            moving = appeared & disappeared or appeared"
L "            if moving:"
L "                mc = min(moving)"
L "                if self.player_color is None:"
L "                    self.player_color = mc"
L "                pos = detect_player_pos(prev_frame, cur_frame)"
L "                if pos:"
L "                    self.player_trail.append(pos)"
L ""
L "        if action == 6 and data:"
L "            x, y = int(data.get('x', 32)), int(data.get('y', 32))"
L "            r, c = min(y, prev_frame.shape[0] - 1), min(x, prev_frame.shape[1] - 1)"
L "            cb, ca = int(prev_frame[r, c]), int(cur_frame[r, c])"
L "            key = (x, y)"
L "            self.toggle_map.setdefault(key, []).append(ca)"
L "            is_toggle = len(set(self.toggle_map[key])) >= 2"
L "            self.click_effs.append({"
L "                'x': x, 'y': y, 'before': cb, 'after': ca,"
L "                'changed': changed, 'diff': diff_count, 'toggle': is_toggle, 'step': step,"
L "            })"
L ""
L "    def get_state(self, last_frame):"
L "        bg = bg_color(last_frame)"
L "        pc = self.player_color"
L "        player = self.player_trail[-1] if self.player_trail else None"
L ""
L "        color_counts = {}"
L "        for color in np.unique(last_frame):"
L "            if color == bg or (pc is not None and color == pc):"
L "                continue"
L "            cnt = int(np.sum(last_frame == color))"
L "            if 1 <= cnt <= 100:"
L "                color_counts[color] = cnt"
L ""
L "        targets = []"
L "        if color_counts:"
L "            goal_color = min(color_counts, key=color_counts.get)"
L "            targets = [tuple(p) for p in np.argwhere(last_frame == goal_color)]"
L "            for c, cnt in sorted(color_counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1]):"
L "                if c == goal_color:"
L "                    continue"
L "                if len(targets) >= 25:"
L "                    break"
L "                targets.extend([tuple(p) for p in np.argwhere(last_frame == c)])"
L ""
L "        walls = np.zeros((64, 64), dtype=bool)"
L "        return player, targets, walls"
L ""
L "class NavigateSpecialist:"
L "    def __init__(self, cls=None):"
L "        self._stuck = 0"
L "        self._prev_pos = None"
L "        self._dir_cycle = 0"
L ""
T GAM
L "        moves = [a for a in avail if a in [1, 2, 3, 4]]"
L "        if not moves:"
L "            return avail[0], None"
L "        if not player:"
L "            act = moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)]"
L "            self._dir_cycle += 1"
L "            return act, None"
L "        if not targets:"
L "            act = moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)]"
L "            self._dir_cycle += 1"
L "            return act, None"
L "        if self._prev_pos == player:"
L "            self._stuck += 1"
L "        else:"
L "            self._stuck = 0"
L "        self._prev_pos = player"
L "        if self._stuck > 8:"
L "            self._stuck = 0"
L "            self._dir_cycle += 1"
L "            return moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)], None"
L ""
L "        near = min(targets, key=lambda t: abs(t[0] - player[0]) + abs(t[1] - player[1]))"
L "        dist = abs(near[0] - player[0]) + abs(near[1] - player[1])"
L "        path = bfs_path(frame, player, tuple(near), walls)"
L "        if path:"
L "            return path[0], None"
L "        if dist <= 2 and 6 in avail:"
L "            return 6, {'x': int(near[1]), 'y': int(near[0])}"
L "        dr = near[0] - player[0]"
L "        dc = near[1] - player[1]"
L "        preferred = 2 if abs(dr) >= abs(dc) and dr > 0 else \\"
L "                    1 if abs(dr) >= abs(dc) else \\"
L "                    4 if dc > 0 else 3"
L "        if preferred in moves:"
L "            return preferred, None"
L "        return moves[self._dir_cycle % len(moves)], None"
L ""

L "            self._idx += 1"
L "            if (x, y) not in self._clicked:"
L "                self._clicked.add((x, y))"
L "                return 6, {'x': x, 'y': y}"

L "def _looks_like_env_root(d: Path) -> bool:"
L "    '''True when *d* contains <stem>/<version>/metadata.json (game-stem layout).'''"

T GAD
L "def _kaggle_dataset_mounts(inp: Path) -> list[Path]:"
L "    '''All dataset mount dirs under /kaggle/input."
L ""
L "    Kaggle uses several nesting conventions:"
L "      <slug>/                          — standard notebook input"
L "      datasets/<user>/<slug>/          — API-added datasets"
L "      competitions/<competition>/      — competition data"
L "    '''"
L "    mounts: list[Path] = []"
L "    for d in inp.iterdir():"
L "        if not d.is_dir():"
L "            continue"
L "        if d.name == \"datasets\":"
L "            for user_d in d.iterdir():"
L "                if user_d.is_dir():"
L "                    mounts.extend(sd for sd in user_d.iterdir() if sd.is_dir())"
L "        elif d.name == \"competitions\":"
L "            mounts.extend(sd for sd in d.iterdir() if sd.is_dir())"
L "        else:"
L "            mounts.append(d)"
L "    return sorted(mounts)"
L ""
T GAD
L "def _check_mount(ds: Path) -> Path | None:"
L "    '''Return the environment_files path from a dataset mount, or None.'''"
L "    if not ds.is_dir():"
L "        return None"
L "    ef = ds / \"environment_files\""
L "    if ef.is_dir():"
L "        return ef"
L "    if _looks_like_env_root(ds):"
L "        return ds"
L "    return None"
L ""
T GAD
L "def _find_all_env_dirs(inp: Path) -> list[Path]:"
L "    '''Return every environment_files directory found under /kaggle/input.'''"
L "    found: list[Path] = []"
L "    for slug in (\"arc-interactive-community\", \"arc-interactive\"):"
L "        for ds in [inp / slug] + list(inp.glob(f\"datasets/*/{slug}\")):"
L "            hit = _check_mount(ds)"
L "            if hit is not None and hit not in found:"
L "                found.append(hit)"
L "    for d in _kaggle_dataset_mounts(inp):"
L "        hit = _check_mount(d)"
L "        if hit is not None and hit not in found:"
L "            found.append(hit)"
L "    return found"
L ""

L "def _game_id_for_stem(stem: str) -> tuple[str, str]:"
L "    '''Return (game_id, env_dir) for a stem across all ENV_DIRS.'''"
L "    for d in ENV_DIRS:"
L "        base = Path(d) / stem"
L "        if not base.is_dir():"
L "            continue"
L "        for vd in sorted(base.iterdir(), key=lambda p: p.name.lower()):"
L "            if not vd.is_dir():"
L "                continue"
L "            mp = vd / \"metadata.json\""
L "            if mp.is_file():"
L "                data = json.loads(mp.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))"
L "                gid = data.get(\"game_id\")"
L "                if isinstance(gid, str):"
L "                    return gid, str(d)"
L "    raise FileNotFoundError(f\"stem {stem!r} not found in {[str(d) for d in ENV_DIRS]}\")"

L "# --- from arc-agi-3-interactive-testbed-200-games.ipynb cell 12 ---"
L "def _terminal(obs) -> bool:"
L "    if obs is None:"
L "        return True"
L "    st = getattr(obs, \"state\", None)"
L "    name = getattr(st, \"name\", \"\") if st is not None else \"\""
L "    return name in (\"WIN\", \"LOSE\", \"LOST\", \"GAME_OVER\")"
L ""

L "def _looks_like_env_root(d: Path) -> bool:"
L "    '''True when *d* contains <stem>/<version>/metadata.json (game-stem layout).'''"
L "    return any(True for _ in d.glob(\"*/*/metadata.json\"))"
L ""
T GAD
L "def _kaggle_dataset_mounts(inp: Path) -> list[Path]:"
L "    '''All dataset mount dirs under /kaggle/input."
L ""
L "    Kaggle uses several nesting conventions:"
L "      <slug>/                          — standard notebook input"
L "      datasets/<user>/<slug>/          — API-added datasets"
L "      competitions/<competition>/      — competition data"
L "    '''"
L "    mounts: list[Path] = []"
L "    for d in inp.iterdir():"
L "        if not d.is_dir():"
L "            continue"
L "        if d.name == \"datasets\":"
L "            for user_d in d.iterdir():"
L "                if user_d.is_dir():"
L "                    mounts.extend(sd for sd in user_d.iterdir() if sd.is_dir())"
L "        elif d.name == \"competitions\":"
L "            mounts.extend(sd for sd in d.iterdir() if sd.is_dir())"
L "        else:"
L "            mounts.append(d)"
L "    return sorted(mounts)"
L ""
T GAD
L "def _check_mount(ds: Path) -> Path | None:"
L "    '''Return the environment_files path from a dataset mount, or None.'''"
L "    if not ds.is_dir():"
L "        return None"
L "    ef = ds / \"environment_files\""
L "    if ef.is_dir():"
L "        return ef"
L "    if _looks_like_env_root(ds):"
L "        return ds"
L "    return None"
L ""
T GAD
L "def _find_all_env_dirs(inp: Path) -> list[Path]:"
L "    '''Return every environment_files directory found under /kaggle/input.'''"
L "    found: list[Path] = []"
L "    for slug in (\"arc-interactive-community\", \"arc-interactive\"):"
L "        for ds in [inp / slug] + list(inp.glob(f\"datasets/*/{slug}\")):"
L "            hit = _check_mount(ds)"
L "            if hit is not None and hit not in found:"
L "                found.append(hit)"
L "    for d in _kaggle_dataset_mounts(inp):"
L "        hit = _check_mount(d)"
